He mentions the Qur'an's warning against wronging an orphan girl in one's care ([[passage/quran-4-3|al-Nisa 3]]), and tells that a man came to [[person/umar-ibn-al-khattab|Umar]] wanting to divorce his wife because he did not love her and Umar said were homes built only on love, where are care and regard (a story without a chain). He says love is in God's hand while affection, mercy, tranquillity and patience are in ours ([[passage/quran-30-21|al-Rum 21]]). This is the lecturer's view and story. (needs_source: the story of Umar with the man has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:27:30}
- He calls on husband and wife each to begin with himself and ask why am I a stranger, and not to put himself in a closed box saying I do not err, he who is with me is a saint and he who is against me is Iblis, and mentions that the Prophet gave the first fruit to a child to mend his feelings (in sense, without a chain), and calls for mending the feelings of the child, the orphan and the woman, and asks each why do you say my wife is no longer what she was. This is the lecturer's guidance. (needs_source: the report of the Prophet giving the first fruit to a child has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:31:00}
- Calls: Nur al-Din from Libya complains of a rebellious wife after childbirth and the lecturer promises an episode on the rebellion of wife and husband; Umm Muhammad from Dubai, whose mother criticises her before people and wrote something for her brother; Umm Omar from Italy asks prayer for her unmarried sister of thirty-six; Muhammad from Dubai asks whether there is a knowledge obligatory on every Muslim; Adel from Dubai, whose wife asks him to return to Egypt; Manal from America asks how homes can be safe in exile; Hamdan from Abu Dhabi says the wife disregards her husband's times and has friends in the home; and Adel from Saudi Arabia says the wife's parents encourage her to harm his mother. Personal details come from text partly garbled. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:41:00}
- To the one whose mother criticises her he says God does not hold her to account for the lack of warmth in her heart but it must not show on her face and she must not say 'uff' to her ([[passage/quran-17-23|al-Isra 23]]), that everyone has a trial and hers is in her mother so let her be patient, and that advice is heavy so she should not make it a dispute. He tells one who blames over everything that he will find no friend, and says God charged the obligations and not the virtues, so she may ask her due right in maintenance and treatment. This is the lecturer's answer; some phrases are garbled. (needs_source: some phrases are garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:44:30}
- To Sarah from Abu Dhabi who fears marriage from the problems of couples she sees he says that when one comes whose religion and character she approves, after istikhara, she should accept, mentions a saying of an Arab woman about men and women (the wording is unclear), cites the verse of affection and mercy ([[passage/quran-30-21|al-Rum 21]]) and the verse on those who leave weak offspring ([[passage/quran-4-9|al-Nisa 9]]), and prays for Umm Omar's sister of thirty-six. See [[practice/istikhara|istikhara]]. This is the lecturer's answer. (needs_source: the saying of the Arab woman is unclear and the report has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:46:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:47:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:47:30}
- To Adel, whose wife asks him to return to Egypt, he says seeking provision is permitted but if it harms the wife and children there is no need for it, and provision is in the heavens ([[passage/quran-51-22|al-Dhariyat 22]]) and its doors do not close, and calls him to return. To the caller whose wife insults his mother he says no one accepts this, so he should warn her, speak to her family and bring an arbiter or a learned man, and if she does not fear God part from her kindly, and does not say divorce her on the air. He promises an episode for families living outside their lands. See [[concept/talaq|divorce]]. This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:47:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:48:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:48:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:49:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:49:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep31 00:50:00}

## Episode 32: contentment in the home
- He asks why we do not glimpse contentment in the faces of old and young in the Muslim home, and holds that a person's feeling of persecution and that many envy him is answered by the hundred others who do not envy him, that one sincere prayer is enough to cover him and ward off envy, and that the sincere Muslim seeks God's pleasure whether people are pleased or not, mentioning verses on affection which are not reproduced. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:03:30}
- Of one who complains that people hate him or that his wife is a routine scowler or his son is not content though his father spends on him in the best way, he says the proverb holds that the killed is pleased and the killer is not, and describes the daughter filled with emptiness who falls into frustration and laziness. Then of the widow he says God either provides one to maintain her, gives her patience, or makes a way for her, and of the divorced woman that either she is wronged and God compensates her or she is wrong and God stopped her wrong, so she should be content. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:07:30}
- He says the content looks on the world with the eye of love and the discontented with the eye of hatred, so he finds fresh water bitter, and makes the first question for contentment who am I, I am God's servant, likening a person to the newborn whom his family turns over, feeds and washes, so we are in God's care (and to God belongs the highest example). He mentions that God gave us Islam without our asking, and tells that al-Shafi'i asked God to grant him Islam and then Paradise (in sense, without a chain). This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the prayer of al-Shafi'i is given in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:10:00}
- He says the scholars of the hearts said manage but do not over-manage, that a person asks about the aim of his life, that among the signs of God's pleasure is that acceptance is laid down for the servant on earth, that others' rancour is envy, that if you are stabbed from behind you are in front, and that the fruiting tree is the one stoned, and repeats be among people like a tree they stone and it throws them fruit (without a chain). This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the saying of the scholars of the hearts and the tree saying have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:13:30}
- He mentions that God's decree removed [[person/yusuf|Yusuf]] from his father's arms, he was sold for a few coins and jailed unjustly, then became manager of the economic crisis in the famine ([[passage/quran-12-47|Yusuf 47]] to [[passage/quran-12-49|49]]), and says a person should not wait for the rulings to be explained to him but should fear God and the way out is opened for him ([[passage/quran-65-2|al-Talaq 2]]). This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:15:30}
- He says Musa's mother cast him into the river and God bound her heart with contentment, certainty and reliance ([[passage/quran-28-10|al-Qasas 10]]), that he grew up thirty years in Pharaoh's palace as training in dealing with the elite (his claim), then ten years as a shepherd in Midian in a calm that teaches patience, then forty years after the casting his Lord said I have made you for Myself ([[passage/quran-20-13|Ta-Ha 13]]) and sent him to Pharaoh. He mentions that [[person/asiya|Asiya]], Pharaoh's wife, was content and prayed my Lord build me a house with You in Paradise ([[passage/quran-66-11|al-Tahrim 11]]) and that they said she chose the neighbour before the house, and that God returned him to his mother so her eye might be cooled ([[passage/quran-28-13|al-Qasas 13]]). This is the lecturer's view and interpretation. (needs_source: the length of Musa's stay in the palace and the interpretation of choosing the neighbour before the house have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:20:00}
- He says the eye is cooled only by contentment, and whoever wakes safe in his home, healthy in his body, with his day's food, it is as if the world were gathered for him (in sense), and calls on one who rebels over clothing or an allowance to look at his brother in Palestine or Iraq who is unsafe, and at those who die of hunger, and to keep good neighbourhood with blessings ([[passage/quran-106-4|Quraysh 4]] and [[passage/quran-16-112|al-Nahl 112]]), for whoever is not content will not be content and the blessing is taken from him. He likens contentment to the launch pad of a rocket. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the report on one who wakes safe in his home, the saying of Isa and the report on neighbourhood with blessings have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:23:00}
- Calls: Amal from Saudi Arabia, wronged by her family, asks how to raise her children not to be angry with their maternal relatives; a mother mentions a daughter of twenty-one who left commitment; Umm Ubayda asks prayer and asks about a sister in God married to a man of very different temperament; Saba from Saudi Arabia; Marwa from Dubai, married three years and not yet given a child, wants contentment; Lamya from Saudi Arabia asks prayer for a righteous husband and two episodes on cutting kinship and on in-laws; a woman tied by contract only to a suitor of troubles and lies; Adel from Abu Dhabi asks about the marriage of his brother who had a prior relationship; and Malika from Italy asks how a person knows that his Lord is pleased with him. Personal details come from text partly garbled. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:33:30}
- To Amal he says a brother's wrong is harder on the soul than the blow of a sword, but we do not answer wrong with severance, rather whoever disobeys God in us we obey God in him and pray for him, and we do not bequeath rancour to our children, and enmities and cutting kinship narrow the world. To the one whose daughter left commitment he says do not press her hard, since advice is heavy and should not be made a dispute or thrown like a mountain, and try through a virtuous woman or a scholar. To the one married to a man of different temperament he says some temperaments change and some do not, like sleeping little or much, citing a verse of [[person/amr-ibn-kulthum|Amr ibn Kulthum]] on pride without quoting it, and reminds (in sense) that all people are of Adam and Adam of dust. This is the lecturer's answer. (needs_source: the report 'all of you are of Adam' is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:37:00}
- To Saba he says aspirations are in the measure of those of resolve and the most tried of people are the prophets (in sense), so let her be patient, seek reward and seek God's help. To Marwa he says God grants females to whom He wills and males to whom He wills or pairs them males and females and makes whom He wills barren ([[passage/quran-42-49|al-Shura 49]] and [[passage/quran-42-50|50]]), so it is a gift from God coming at the time He wills, and mentions [[person/zakariyya|Zakariyya]], who was about ninety, and called on his Lord ([[passage/quran-19-5|Maryam 5]] to [[passage/quran-19-7|7]]). This is the lecturer's answer. (needs_source: the report on the prophets being the most tried is in sense without a chain and Zakariyya's age has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:41:00}
- To Lamya he promises two separate episodes on cutting kinship and on in-laws because cutting kin prevents provision and removes its blessing (in sense, without a chain). To the woman tied by contract only to a lying man he says the liar brings the far near and the near far and if he lies to you he lies to God, and holds that she should free herself from him if it is proved that his deeds do not match what was said, after istikhara and consulting her family (his opinion). For Adel he prays that God provide his brother with good. This is the lecturer's answer. (needs_source: the statement that cutting kinship prevents provision has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:43:00}
- To Malika, who asks how a person knows that his Lord is pleased with him, he says among the signs is people's love, tells (in sense) that when God loves a servant He calls Jibril to love him and calls among the angels and acceptance is laid down for him on earth, and the reverse in hatred, and that among the signs is that obedience becomes beloved and easy and not heavy. This is the lecturer's answer. (needs_source: the report of Jibril's love is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:44:00}
- He ends on raising children to contentment: the art of listening, so all listen to the four-year-old when he speaks and to the elder, keeping them from trivialities, and planting high aspiration, mentioning verses on the falcon which are not reproduced, and tells (in sense) of [[person/ibn-al-jawzi|Ibn al-Jawzi]] saying if your aspiration takes you to heaven do not settle for earth, and his symbolic story of water and olive oil, which rose above it after the grinding and pressing. He promises an episode on contentment with God and God's contentment with us. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the saying of Ibn al-Jawzi and the story of water and oil have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:46:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:47:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep32 00:47:30}

## Episode 33: mutual respect in the home
- He devotes the episode to the lost mutual respect, saying respect used to show toward the head of the family through his deeds and not his words, his calm and not his anger, his example and not his orders, that a wife's respect for her husband is not coercion or suppression, that in some women a narcissistic personality swells so they look down on their husbands and taunt them with their favours, and that in the former generation all stood for the father when he entered out of love of God and not fear whereas today the boy lies on his sofa without moving and may answer his father with a look of disdain or say that he is deficient in his education. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:05:00}
- He presents the complaint of a husband who says his wife does not respect him after he used to confide his secrets to her in the first ten years, so she taunts him with them and brings them out of the 'black box' at the hour of quarrel to win. He tells husbands that whoever's chest is tight with his secret, another's chest is tighter (in sense), and that whoever leaves the way weakens his grip on God's rope ([[passage/quran-3-103|Al Imran 103]]) and relations grow weak. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the saying 'whoever's chest is tight with his secret' is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:08:00}
- He listens to the husband who justifies his lack of trust by his wife's intense jealousy, and says excessive jealousy is folly in man and woman and praiseworthy jealousy is for the sanctities of God, mentioning (in sense) the Prophet's saying do you wonder at Sa'd's jealousy, [[person/sad-ibn-ubada|Sa'd ibn Ubada]], God is more jealous than he. He tells that [[person/ali-ibn-abi-talib|Ali]] was jealous for [[person/fatima|Fatima]] over a toothstick and addressed it in verses which are not reproduced (a story without a chain). He says if you doubt do not investigate (a saying in sense) and that the master of the people is the one who feigns dullness. These are the lecturer's views and stories. (needs_source: the report on Sa'd's jealousy, the story of Ali and the toothstick and the saying 'if you doubt do not investigate' have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:12:00}
- He explains ignoring then forgiving: the wise advising father ignores the first and second mistake and then alerts the boy so the message is strong, and says in homes we are not a monitoring body like a traffic policeman but a body of embrace and containment, and whoever pardons God pardons him, whoever lowers his eyes from people's faults God lowers people's eyes from his faults and veils him (in sense), and describes one who sees faults as having a telescope while blind to merits. This is the lecturer's guidance. (needs_source: the reports 'whoever pardons' and 'whoever lowers his eyes' are in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:15:00}
- He tells that [[person/al-jahiz|al-Jahiz]] passed a tent and saw an ugly man scolding his wife, who was of fair face and spoke gently to him, and when he asked her she said perhaps he did a good deed and I was its fruit and perhaps I did a bad one and he was its fruit, and I hope we shall be in Paradise, he by his thanks to God that He gave him one like me and I by my patience with him. He infers the sense of this woman. This is a story without a source. (needs_source: the story of al-Jahiz and the tent has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:18:00}
- To the woman proud toward her husband for her lineage and beauty he says all people are of Adam and Adam of dust (in sense), that a woman who looks down loses her credit, and relates a mother's counsel to her daughter be earth to him and he will be sky to you, be a bed to him and he will be a cover to you, be a servant to him and he will be a slave to you (without a chain), holding that this is not humiliation but the dignity of one who knows her limits, that wealth and beauty are a passing trifle, and that the ugly one is the unbeliever and the wicked. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the report 'all of you are of Adam' and the mother's counsel have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:21:30}
- To the wife who complains of her husband's lack of respect for her he tells the husband to recall that his wife stood by him and bore with him, to honour her before her daughters and sons, and to ask himself whether he would accept it for his daughter, and says it is permissible lying for a man to praise his wife for what she has, and faults denying favours and belittling the woman who may be more successful than he. This is the lecturer's view; he gave no evidence for the permissible lying. (needs_source: the ruling on permissible lying has no evidence) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:23:30}
- Of the irritable wife he says we look at the symptom and do not treat the disease like one who gives aspirin for a headache, and that her irritability may come from menopause, fear, a rebellious daughter, a problem with her family or from him, so he should look for the roots of the nerves' tension, and he advises the tense person to calm for what it does to his health. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:26:30}
- He tells the husband who flares up because furniture was moved that the house is everyone's and the wife has a say in arranging it and he should not meddle in trifles, tells the wife not to mock her husband before the children nor put her finger on the wound, and tells the righteous woman who attends gatherings of knowledge to thank God that her husband does not smoke or do wrong but likes football, and not to mock his hobby. This is the lecturer's guidance. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:30:00}
- He says the Arab husband is a big child whom a kind word pleases and softens his heart and who does not like anyone to look down on him, and calls the sincere Muslim to breadth of outlook. This is the lecturer's generalisation about the Arab husband. (needs_source: the generalisation about the Arab husband has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:31:30}
- He tells that the Prophet went in to [[person/fatima|Fatima]] and said where is your cousin, and she said I was angry with him and he went out, and he found [[person/ali-ibn-abi-talib|Ali]] asleep in the mosque with dust on him, brushed it off and said rise, Abu Turab, took his hand and brought him in to Fatima without asking either of them about the dispute, and says this kindness plants credit and increases respect. This is the lecturer's story in sense, without a chain. (needs_source: the story of the Prophet, Ali, Fatima and Abu Turab is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:34:00}
- He speaks of the tendency of some wives to control, saying that the yielding husband hands her the reins of the house and she tires since a wife by nature likes to be a follower as the perfection of her femininity (his view), cites the verse on finding rest in her ([[passage/quran-30-21|al-Rum 21]]), and says be mistress of the house, not its president. He mentions another kind of husband who does not accept anyone controlling him so he belittles her or marries another or wastes time with friends, so homes become tombs in which the dead live in the form of the living, and the spider's house ([[passage/quran-29-41|al-Ankabut 41]]). This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:37:00}
- He says if the wife counted her husband's mother a second mother and the husband his wife's mother a second mother, matters would settle, that part of respect is that each side respect the other's wishes in food so we do not impose what he dislikes, and that some husbands complain that their wife lacks qualities of motherhood, her voice raised on the children. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:39:30}
- He says other parties have a part in the loss of respect, as some mothers tell their son divorce your wife, and answers one who cites the story of [[person/ibrahim|Ibrahim]], who told [[person/ismail|Ismail]] to change the threshold of his door, and of [[person/abu-bakr|Abu Bakr]], who told his son [[person/abdullah-ibn-abi-bakr|Abdullah]] to divorce his wife and then took him back to her when he saw him weeping, that there is no analogy because these are not like others, and that the better thing is for the father to tell his son look after your wife and be kind and not to order divorce. See [[concept/talaq|divorce]]. This is the lecturer's view and stories. (needs_source: the story of Abu Bakr and his son Abdullah has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:41:30}
- He closes by saying spouses should not set their mother and father in one pan and their partner in the other, since the mother has one kind of rights and the wife another and it is not right to set them in two pans (as in an earlier episode), and reminds that in homes we are friends of God and not enemies, that the husband is a cover and veil for his wife, and of the solemn covenant. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep33 00:43:30}

## Episode 34: quarrel and harmony in the home
- He begins with the saying that good character builds nations, ascribes to [[person/al-ghazali-abu-hamid|Abu Hamid al-Ghazali]] that good character is faith and bad character is hypocrisy, and says quarrel tightens the chest and the world for all its breadth does not hold two who quarrel while a needle's eye holds those who love, mentioning a verse on this which is not reproduced, and that the eye of love hides faults and the eye of hatred shows defects. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the saying of al-Ghazali is given with no work named) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:02:30}
- He sets out Islam's view of the human soul: no soul is wronged at all on the Day of Resurrection ([[passage/quran-21-47|al-Anbiya 47]]), tells (in sense) that the Prophet stood for the funeral of a Jew and when told he was a Jew said is it not a soul, and that God is Lord of all the worlds, then says that quarrel lets in backbiting and tale-bearing so good deeds are lost and sins multiply. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the report of standing for the Jew's funeral is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:05:00}
- He mentions that quarrel turns kind words into other meanings, and tells that [[person/umar-ibn-abd-al-aziz|Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz]] stepped on a man asleep in the mosque before dawn who said are you blind and he said no and stopped his entourage from punishing him, and that [[person/al-hasan-al-basri|al-Hasan al-Basri]] saw boys drumming on a boat on the day of the feast and prayed for them, O God as You gladdened them in the world have mercy on them in the hereafter. These are the lecturer's stories, without chains. (needs_source: stories and sayings without a chain or source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:07:30}
- He says quarrel makes a person one of the short-measurers ([[passage/quran-83-1|al-Mutaffifin 1]]) in dealing with people, treating his son's wife differently from his daughter's. Then he gives figures on wars: that in five thousand years up to 1945 more than thirty-four thousand battles took place at six a year, that the Prophet's campaigns were about twenty-seven expeditions and thirty-eight raiding parties and that the dead on both sides were about one and a half per cent of the fighters, whereas tens of millions died in the Second World War, and that the dead of his campaigns numbered a few hundred (the figures are garbled in the text). These are the lecturer's claims. (needs_source: the figures on battles and dead have no source and the text is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:12:00}
- On the effect of quarrel on children he says the mother who does not know smiling and the father who knows only a raised hand and miserliness bring out psychologically warped children, who after years make their fiancé or fiancée taste evil torment, and when divorce occurs stories are spread before friends and families, so harmony is kinder than quarrel. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:14:00}
- He mentions sources that feed quarrel, first pride: that the other will not accept my opinion and answers me back, so the seed of quarrel grows. He tells (in sense) that the Prophet used to spread his face to the one before him, that a girl would take his hand and he would go with her, that on returning from a campaign he played with the children and took [[person/abdullah-ibn-jafar|Abdullah ibn Ja'far]] up behind him, that a camel's owner was told it complained, that a lark grieved for its chicks and he ordered them returned, and that one who shows no mercy is shown none. This is the lecturer's view and stories. (needs_source: stories and sayings without a chain or source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:19:00}
- He says (in sense) that whoever kills a gecko with the first blow has great reward but one must not torment it, and contrasts this with the savagery he describes in Bosnia in 1992 and the extermination of the Native Americans by spreading diseases, saying we have no such savagery. These are the lecturer's claims. (needs_source: the report in sense and the claims on Bosnia and the Native Americans have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:20:30}
- He asks the quarrelling spouses on what basis they married when the registrar said I marry you on the Book of God and the Sunna of His Messenger, and whether wrong, hurt and killing initiative are of the Book and Sunna, and whether if the bearer of the message visited our homes he would enter glad, and recalls the episodes on the jurisprudence of taste and not raising the voice at the other. This is the lecturer's guidance. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:22:30}
- He mentions that quarrel brings nerve illness and high pressure and sends the man to the cafe and the club, and tells (in sense) that the Prophet said to [[person/aisha|Aisha]] I know when you are pleased with me and when you are angry, when pleased you say no by the Lord of Muhammad and when angry no by the Lord of Ibrahim, and she said I only abandon your name. He tells (in sense) that Iblis places his throne on the water and gathers his sons and gives the crown of devilry to the one who separated a couple. He says one who divorces blames his wife and does not admit his fault (his claim). This is the lecturer's view and stories. (needs_source: the reports on Aisha and on Iblis's throne are in sense without a chain and the claim about those who divorce has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:27:00}
- He says the kind word is charity and smiling in your brother's face is charity, that looking down on people is unfitting since all of us are of Adam and Adam of dust and there is no merit of an Arab over a non-Arab save by piety (in sense), and that Iblis asked us to expose our women and to hate one another and we responded. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the reports are in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:29:00}
- He mentions (in sense) the saying that whoever wakes safe in his home, healthy in his body, with his day's food, it is as if the world were gathered for him, and says how can he be safe in his home who has in his house one who does not make him safe, and prays for security for Muslim lands, says the deaf have a right to programmes translated into sign language, and that what ruins our lives is living beyond our means, and calls on us to make peace with ourselves first and then with people, and to seek for our brother an excuse, up to seventy excuses. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the report on one who wakes safe and the seventy excuses are in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:32:30}
- Calls: a woman says her husband's family are hostile to hers after the quarrel and asks for a word to the parties; Umm Ubayd says that in doubt about his wife her husband said you are divorced three times in one sitting and asks what to do; Umm Saif asks about an irritable husband of great jealousy; and others. The lecturer speaks of families splitting into two camps after divorce and holds this has nothing to do with Islam, since the two families chose each other after istikhara and consultation. The text is partly garbled and some lines are cut. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:40:30}
- Abd al-Mun'im asks whether quarrel is a fruit of distance from the Book of God and suggests a prize for memorising the Qur'an, Umm Hasan asks about a husband who made his wife's divorce conditional on seeing her do something, and Aliya asks the ruling on defaming a divorced woman. The lecturer answers that defaming a divorced woman is a sin, that rulings are not built on doubt but on certainty, that divorce three times in one sitting counts as one divorce (this being a jurisprudence question other programmes answer), and that one who finds a matter that is ninety-nine per cent likely wrong and one per cent likely right should take it as right. This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. (needs_source: the ruling on three divorces in one sitting has no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:44:30}
- To the woman who complains of her husband's irritability he says irritability is like a volcano and an earthquake, so let one say Allahu akbar and calm. Ali complains that he loves hearing the Qur'an and praying but something distracts him at its time, and a caller says her husband prays only the Friday prayer and his character is good. He tells Ali that setting the day's balance by the dawn prayer in the mosque sets his day, and tells the wife to be patient and pray for him, for perhaps he did not grow up in his family's home with anyone encouraging him to pray. This is the lecturer's answer. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:46:30}
- To the woman whose husband, after she found him out in something bad, said if you find out another you are divorced, he says divorce made conditional on a condition takes effect according to some jurists and not according to others, and it is best that he be upright on God's way. On defaming the divorced woman he says it is no one's right, and tells that a man who wanted to divorce his wife was asked why and said a Muslim does not bring out his home's secrets, and when she married another he said how can I speak of another's wife, and whoever defames a person God defames him (in sense, without a chain). This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. (needs_source: the story of the divorcing man and the saying on defaming have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:47:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:47:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:48:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:48:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:49:00}
- He ends by saying a husband who did not grow up in his family's home with one to encourage him to pray prays it as a duty and feels he is rid of it, whereas one accustomed from childhood is glad to hear the muezzin, asks whether we like to live in quarrel or in harmony, and says intention must turn into deed, and that an institution whose people love one another becomes a garden. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:49:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:50:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:50:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep34 00:51:00}

## Episode 35: the universal language, the language of ethics
- He speaks of a universal language mastered by the Prophet's Companions, not spoken by the tongue but by conduct, the language of ethics, for if we ask any human being whether he loves justice and mercy and hates wrong he says yes, and says 'read' divided an age in which people were far from the teachings of the Lord of heaven from the age of being guided by them, and that some people have not changed the ethics of the Jahiliyya. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:03:00}
- He mentions the burying of girls in the Jahiliyya, then says today the daughters of ideas are buried, so we bury the other's idea and belittle him and rancour grows, so a brother cannot love his brother nor a wife be at ease with her husband, and calls on fathers to make room for their children's discussion and to come down to the ground on which they stand, and says (a saying he ascribes with no source) raise your children for their time and not yours. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the saying 'raise your children for their time' has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:06:30}
- He says upbringing fifty or a hundred years ago involved everyone, grandfather, uncle, maternal uncle and teacher, and that one may find a person who cannot write his name yet is refined in the language of manners and listening, and tells (in sense) that the Prophet gave people their places and disliked reserving spots in the mosque and sat where the gathering ended for him. He dislikes suppression, mentions (in sense) that in Hell there is a valley in which is put one who brought grief upon his family, and criticises a father who forces his daughter to marry a rich man without regard to his character or her consent. This is the lecturer's view and stories. (needs_source: the reports in sense and the valley of Hell have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:08:30}
- He explains the orphan as he understands it: among humans one whose father died before puberty, among animals one whose mother died, among birds one whose parents both died (his definition), and says the best house is one in which an orphan is honoured, and that an orphan may be an orphan of feelings if his parents are alive but his mother is busy and his father sees him only from time to time, citing a line of verse which is not reproduced. This is the lecturer's view and definition. (needs_source: the definition of the orphan among animals and birds has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:11:00}
- He tells (in sense) that when the Prophet came out of the mosque with [[person/safiyya-bint-huyayy|Safiyya]] he knelt so that she might put her foot on his knees and mount the camel, and says this is guardianship and containment, not muscles or a tongue, and that whoever uses his guardianship to humiliate the weak is like the strangled and the beaten, and the strong is he who controls himself at anger (in sense). See [[concept/al-qiwama|guardianship]]. This is the lecturer's view and story. (needs_source: the account of the Prophet kneeling for Safiyya and the report on the strong at anger are in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:13:30}
- He tells (in sense) that the wife of [[person/umar-ibn-al-khattab|Umar]] answered him back on a matter and he objected, and she said do you object while your daughter [[person/hafsa|Hafsa]] answers the Prophet back until he is angry all day, so he went to his daughter to ask her and was astonished, and says the Prophet was a human being who is patient and angers. This is the lecturer's story, without a chain. (needs_source: the story of Umar, his wife and Hafsa has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:14:30}
- He tells that [[person/abu-al-as-ibn-al-rabi|Abu al-As ibn al-Rabi]], husband of the Prophet's daughter [[person/zaynab-bint-muhammad|Zaynab]], was taken captive at Badr while still an unbeliever, and the Prophet heard at the dawn prayer the voice of Zaynab saying I have granted him protection, and told the Companions this is Zaynab's voice and accepted her protection, and she ransomed him with her mother Khadija's necklace, at which the Prophet wept when he saw it and said if you wish to release her captive, and he draws three lessons: the Prophet's attachment to his daughter, his honouring her by accepting her protection, and the example of listening to the other's request. See [[event/battle-of-badr|Badr]]. This is the lecturer's account, in sense and without a chain. (needs_source: the story of Zaynab and Abu al-As is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:17:30}
- He mentions Jahili partisanship and says part of partisanship is a father saying his daughter is an angel who does not err or his son is an angel who does not err, while the one who does not err is the Messenger and all the sons of Adam err, and tells (in sense) support your brother wrong or wronged, by restraining him from his wrong. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the report 'support your brother' is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:19:30}
- He criticises those who weep at the dawn prayer and remind of God on Friday while at home treating their family with the conduct of Abu Jahl, and tells that a woman came to the Prophet wanting divorce and said I do not fault him in character or religion but I dislike disbelief within Islam, and he asked her husband will you take back his garden and she said yes, so she returned the garden and the matter ended in minutes (in sense), contrasts it with years of waiting in the courts, mentions that the divorce rate in some states is from thirty-six to fifty-six per cent (his claim), and holds that the Zahiris require witnesses for divorce. See [[person/abu-jahl|Abu Jahl]]. This is the lecturer's view and story. (needs_source: the story of the woman and her garden is in sense, and the divorce rate and the Zahiri view have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:22:30}
- He tells the man who cannot harmonise with his wife after doing all he can to release her kindly, and that souls are conscripted soldiers, those that know one another unite and those that are strangers differ (in sense), and tells a story of a sparrow and a crow brought together by misfortune. He reminds that the prayer of the wronged has no veil between it and God (in sense) and ruins the wrongdoer's life, and that God says to the wronged I will help you even after a while. This is the lecturer's view and stories. (needs_source: stories and sayings without a chain or source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:25:30}
- He mentions that God gave as a similitude two wives of prophets who disbelieved ([[passage/quran-66-10|al-Tahrim 10]]) and a believing woman whose husband was a tyrant ([[passage/quran-66-11|al-Tahrim 11]]), and says souls harmonise when behaviours draw near, that wrong from the strong to the weak removes blessing from provision and children, and tells (in sense) the Prophet's charge to [[person/abu-dharr-al-ghifari|Abu Dharr al-Ghifari]] to make his ship sound, increase his provision and make his intention sincere, and that God looks not at your forms but at your hearts and deeds. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the Prophet's charge to Abu Dharr and the report 'He does not look at your forms' are in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:28:30}
- He says the believer is like the bee which takes only the nectar of flowers and feeds honey in which is healing and when it settles on a twig does not break it, so he is not heavy and does not tighten people's chests but opens them. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:30:00}
- Calls: Umm Ubayda from Sharjah finds her husband of good character but no common language after four or five years, and he does not accept advice, and they have two daughters; Munira from Saudi Arabia asks for his number; Saad from Iraq asks for advice for teachers and students; Umm Muhammad, married twenty-five years, asks about the harmony of souls; Sumayya from Tunisia, pregnant, asks prayer; Mustafa asks whether a human being is free or compelled; Hanan from Dubai, whose husband is a doctor to whom women patients send greeting messages; a woman from Morocco on divorce by three; Aisha from Dubai on the delay of her marriage contract over a word; a woman whose husband does not support their two children; Abu Abdullah from al-Ain on charity for deceased parents; Tamara from Abu Dhabi wants to divorce a bad husband and her father does not agree; and Abdullah from Saudi Arabia. Personal details come from text partly garbled. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:45:00}
- To Muhammad from Libya, who cannot please his mother, he says some sons lose the key to the gate of the mother's heart, her heart is tender, and no anger, frown or waving off of the hand should show on his face. This is the lecturer's answer. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:35:00}
- To Umm Ubayda he says to seek reward and be patient then open her chest and not try to shake an entrenched temperament, for the irritable will not change, nor the miser budge, nor the sarcastic, unless the fear and love of God enter his heart, and if her patience runs out and she fears for her health and life they may part after exhausting the means (his view, no evidence). To Saad he says teachers should take their pupils as sons and pupils should fear God in their teachers because the teacher shields you from the fire of the hereafter (his claim). To Umm Muhammad he says the reward of the patient is great, and to Mustafa that a human being is free in what he will be accounted for and compelled in what he is not, such as when he is born, dies and falls ill, citing a saying he ascribes to [[person/jafar-al-sadiq|Ja'far al-Sadiq]] with no source. This is the lecturer's answer. (needs_source: the ruling on parting has no evidence, and the saying of Ja'far al-Sadiq and the claim that the teacher shields from the fire have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:46:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:47:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:47:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:48:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:48:30}
- To Hanan, whose husband the doctor receives messages from women patients, he says the doctor's task is in the clinic inside the hospital and ends there, that prayer from afar is better, and that there is no need for messages to a foreign man; to Aisha he says he calls for speed in divorce if both sides want it and that the delay of a marriage contract over a word may be because the judge applies a particular law; and to the woman whose husband does not support their two children he says to be patient and seek reward and that God compensates her. The questions on divorce by three and on charity for deceased parents remain unanswered in the text. This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:49:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:49:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:50:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:50:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep35 00:51:00}

## Episode 36: the spouses' telescopes and their troubles
- He says the rule is that homes are safe and problems the exception, that the reverse is a danger to our society, that if we compare the homes of the early generations and our grandparents with ours we would find their problems rare exceptions, mentions the saying of the jurists of principles that the rare has no ruling, and that many of us treat the symptom and not the disease. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the comparison between the homes of the early generations and ours has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:03:00}
- He calls the illnesses he wants to present social and behavioural, and holds that the greatest handicap is the behavioural handicap: miserliness, foolishness, rashness, sharpness where gentleness is due and softness where firmness is due, pride toward women colleagues at work and meekness at home or the reverse, and social tumours within homes that need treatment. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:04:30}
- He looks at the husband through the wife's telescope and sees him as of little attention, present yet absent, and if she ranked his interests she would find his work first then his dress, food, sport, circle of friends, the cafe, the club and his family, and she is between twelfth and twentieth, and he covers the weakness of his argument by raising his voice like a lawyer with no documents before the judge. This is the lecturer's portrayal and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:07:30}
- He mentions that some husbands put their noses into small and great matters: why is this chair here, though the house is essentially the wife's to arrange, and Satan works against us twenty-four hours. He describes pride as a tumour that swells until a person sees himself in every direction, tells the husband to thank his wife's family and raise her standing before them so he builds credit, not to belittle her before her children, and cites the saying of [[person/umar-ibn-al-khattab|Umar]], O God let no wicked man have a favour on me so that I love him (without a chain). He recalls the saying they are a garment for you and you a garment for them ([[passage/quran-2-187|al-Baqara 187]]) and that both married on the Book and the Sunna. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the saying of Umar on the wicked and a favour has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:13:30}
- Among other illnesses of the husband are being addicted to the screen and looking at what God forbade so problems flare, praising women colleagues before his wife without regard for her jealousy, and he says jealousy is praiseworthy and blameworthy and most of it today is misplaced; surprising her with guests every night while she is worn out by her children and house; burdening homes beyond their means for show before people; thinking himself a genius no one approaches though all of us err; and reading his newspaper turning the sports pages while she speaks of the fate of their son and daughter and her marriage. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:19:30}
- Then he looks through the husband's lens and sees that his wife contradicts him in small and great matters and supports others' views against him, is given to grumbling, goes out and in too often unlike what [[person/umar-ibn-al-khattab|Umar]] described of the girl of Madyan ([[passage/quran-28-25|al-Qasas 25]]), not one who goes in and out much (without a chain), is extravagant, and he puts her forward in large matters then despises her in small, she does not lend him her ear when he complains of work so he feels short in work and at home, and he complains of her lying and her fatal jealousy and her speaking constantly of her family and belittling his. This is the lecturer's portrayal and generalisation. (needs_source: Umar's description of the girl of Madyan has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:25:00}
- Calls: a woman from Australia, two months married, says her husband is ill-mannered and assaulted her and asks about khul', her money and her jewellery; Sami from Germany asks for a special episode on safe homes in Europe where there are thirty million Muslims; Mu'min from Sharjah asks about the constant interference of mothers-in-law; Khalil from Abu Dhabi asks whether a father distributes his inheritance in his lifetime, asks advice for the newly married because of divorce over trivial reasons, and asks the ruling on bank loans; Nagham from al-Ain asks prayer for pregnancy; and Hassan from Saudi Arabia. Personal details come from text partly garbled. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:33:30}
- To the woman whose husband struck her and who left his house with her mother's help he says a woman does not leave her home when angry with her husband, as the home is her homeland, unless the husband is in a state of madness that endangers her life, and says (in sense) the Prophet never struck a woman, and if the two arbiters do not reconcile she asks for divorce for harm, and if the husband refuses she asks for khul' and takes her money because he is the one who gave her what he gave. To one troubled by obsessive whispers who feels he will die and so writes out the Qur'an he says to seek refuge in God from Satan, make ablution and sit in gatherings of knowledge, and to the one who swore by divorce to ask a scholar in his country whether it takes effect. This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa.
