(needs_source: the report that the Prophet never struck a woman is in sense without a chain and the khul' ruling has no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:36:30}
- To Sami he promises a special episode on homes in Europe, and to Umm Ahmad on the interference of mothers-in-law he wishes that the husband's mother and the wife's mother be light-hearted guests, and that each side notice that it often takes a stand against the mother from the engagement. To Khalil he says inheritance comes after death and what a person distributes in his lifetime is a gift in which he is even among the children (his view), and refers to the jurisprudence programmes, and advises the newly married to study carefully for one, two or three years before divorce so each understands the other's environment and upbringing. This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. (needs_source: the ruling that distributing in life is a gift made evenly has no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:40:00}
- He calls for our wealth to be lawful so we do not work where there is doubt nor go to usurious banks nor bring bribery to our children, for God removes the blessing, and tells that [[person/abu-hanifa|Abu Hanifa]], a suckling of three months, was nursed by a girl of their neighbours, and when his father learned their wealth was not lawful he put his finger in his mouth to bring back what he had nursed (a story without a chain). This is the lecturer's view and story. (needs_source: the story of Abu Hanifa and nursing has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:42:30}
- Among the wife's faults to him is neglecting herself and adornment at home and adorning herself at great cost to go out, and he holds that she should wear fine clothes at home for her husband and when going out be modest and covered (his view). He calls on the husband to an economic reckoning: how much he would pay a nanny, a cook, teachers, a washer and a psychiatrist were his wife not doing so, and the wife looks at the scale of her husband's merits of generous character and hand and no harm, so homes draw near, and if it does not avail they part and God enriches each from His bounty. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the view on a woman's dress at home and outside has no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep36 00:45:30}

## Episode 37: Mu'awiya's hair in the home
- He says the episode is about features of character between man and woman, and mentions the saying of [[person/muawiya-ibn-abi-sufyan|Mu'awiya]], between me and people is a hair, if they pull it I loosen it and if they loosen it I pull it (without a chain), and asks where Mu'awiya's hair is among the people of a home while the world gathers in spite of its differences and they scatter. He promises an episode on spinsterhood, saying it has no connection with poverty since its rate in some rich countries is twenty and thirty per cent and in another society one per cent (his claim). This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the saying of Mu'awiya and the percentages on spinsterhood have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:03:00}
- Among the causes is that some people have no wish to develop themselves and spend their lives before the television, and he tells the story of a merchant whom four traders wished to occupy, each meeting him and saying his turban is a colour other than its real colour until he was confused, and says how much more are affected those influenced by others such as some women who lend their ears to friends and mothers so the decision is not their own and the family relation turns to desert. This is the lecturer's story, without a source. (needs_source: the story of the merchant and the turban has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:07:30}
- He advises wives not to chase their husbands with where did you go and what happened, since the Arab man by nature does not accept being chased (his generalisation), and says the manner of presentation matters: if salt is too much in the food the wise husband says the cooking is excellent but the salt seems a little more, and he faults one who says would you serve this to your mother, and even if the wife is a university professor the man wants a cup of tea from her hand. This is the lecturer's guidance. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:10:00}
- He criticises those who dispute much and repeat the subject as if their wife were deficient in mind, and the woman who extinguishes the spark of her husband's enthusiasm by saying are you Einstein or Newton, and calls for encouragement and ignoring faults, for if we reduce disputation, blame and belittling of the other tranquillity descends. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:13:30}
- He says excessive care smothers affection since a man is not a child nor a woman, and there must be room in which the other moves. He tells (in sense) that the Prophet would talk with [[person/aisha|Aisha]] and say I know when you are pleased with me and when you are angry, you say no by the Lord of Muhammad and no by the Lord of Ibrahim, and she says but you are in my heart, and draws from it that he understood her, spoke with her of what is useful and drew her attention. He says many men dislike being chased. This is the lecturer's view and story. (needs_source: the report of Aisha and the Lord of Ibrahim is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:17:00}
- He mentions mood swings in some spouses and says it is better for the other to be silent and seek reward, and compares the grandmothers' lives busy with the house to today's with washing machine, cook and ready food yet brittle bones, rheumatism and psychological sorrow, holding that familiarity with luxury is a calamity, since the girl pampered at her father's cannot run a house. He tells (in sense) that the Prophet was in the service of his family, cutting meat with them, sweeping and bringing salt to Aisha. He faults the husband who puts his nose in the kitchen. This is the lecturer's view and story. (needs_source: the account of the Prophet serving his family is in sense and the illnesses are mentioned with no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:21:00}
- He says not understanding the other's feelings and not valuing them is dangerous, and gives the example that the husband in our lands does not attend the birth of his child and holds that these are customs and traditions treated as if they were of religion, and wants us to develop our mentality and not our religion. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:23:00}
- He says not keeping secrets is one of the calamities of homes: the wife's colleagues learn her secrets and the husband remains as if naked, and likewise the reverse, and mentions the wife of Nuh and the wife of Lut ([[passage/quran-66-10|al-Tahrim 10]]), their betrayal being taking home secrets outside, and calls for our homes not to be like the spider's house ([[passage/quran-29-41|al-Ankabut 41]]). This is the lecturer's view and interpretation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:25:00}
- He asks whether the word I am sorry or I have erred lowers the Arab's dignity, tells (in sense) that a woman said I shall not taste sleep until you are pleased with me and she is in Paradise, and says this does not lessen her. This is the lecturer's view and a garbled story. (needs_source: the story is garbled and has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:26:30}
- Calls: Hayat from Belgium asks off the subject about fear of flying; Abu Jarrah from Abu Dhabi asks about unmarried girls, filling leisure time and teaching children the correct Qur'an; a woman from Dubai whose husband of twenty-five years still contacts another woman after his commitment and forbids her the market; Umm Hanim from Saudi Arabia, whose daughter married without her consent and asks whether her anger is sinful; a man asks about his mother who performs the lesser pilgrimage and fasts much and about mixing in schools after what was said of the mufti of Makkah; Najwa from Syria on youths following a man who claims to interpret dreams; Umm Adel from Abu Dhabi says her mother-in-law prays against her children; and Abd al-Mun'im asks for a book and episodes. Personal details come from text partly garbled. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:35:00}
- To Abu Jarrah he says marriage is provision and we must not close its door with many demands but make it easy for one whose religion and character we approve (in sense), that a woman's leisure is filled by care of the house and a weekly gathering of knowledge instead of serials, and that memorising the Qur'an needs motivation from childhood. To the woman whose husband contacts another woman he says do not leave him nor go to your family's house but be patient as long as he listens to advice, and that his stopping her from the market while she wears the lawful covering may be a sin and calls for room for understanding. This is the lecturer's answer. (needs_source: the report 'if one comes whose religion you approve' is in sense without a chain and the ruling on forbidding going out has no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:40:00}
- To Umm Hanim, whose daughter married without her consent and to a man already married, he says the better thing is that a girl not marry without her parents' consent and that marriage without the guardian's permission may lack blessing since it grieves father and mother, but it is now a fact so they accept it and please her, and adds that half a man is better than no man (his view). To the son whose mother performs the lesser pilgrimage and fasts much he says it is good for the home and to ease it for her gently, and on mixing in schools he says our rules are not necessarily what the matter was (the answer is unclear). See [[concept/wali|guardian]]. This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. (needs_source: the view on marriage without the guardian and its blessing has no evidence and the answer on mixing is unclear) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:43:00}
- To Najwa, whose son of twenty-seven left his family and followed a man claiming to interpret dreams, he says the world becomes dreams for them and we become dependents, and suggests contacting the mufti of Aleppo. To Umm Adel he says a mother's prayer against her son if it is with right God answers, and if it is with wrong and aggression He does not, and prays that the dutiful son be given patience and the stubborn mother be given 'Mu'awiya's hair'. He promises a book and episodes. This is the lecturer's answer. (needs_source: the statement on when a mother's prayer is answered has no evidence) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep37 00:46:30}

## Episode 38: persuasion and conviction through gentleness
- He says gentleness is what lets words penetrate the soul and become conviction, that persuasion by twisting an arm, raising the voice and boasting may pass today and not remain tomorrow, that compulsion belongs only to the commands of the law while ideas and opinion and counter-opinion are for persuasion, and tells that [[person/abd-allah-ibn-al-mubarak|Abd Allah ibn al-Mubarak]] sat beside a man who sneezed and did not say praise be to God, so Ibn al-Mubarak said what does one say who sneezes, and he said praise be to God, and he said may God have mercy on you, and says this is a gentle way of teaching without wounding (a story without a chain). This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the story of Ibn al-Mubarak and the sneeze has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:03:00}
- He tells a story of a guest who passed a tent and saw a slave in bonds and interceded for him with his master, and the master said he had a beautiful voice and chanted to the camels for three days so they hurried until they perished except a lean camel, then the guest asked the slave to let him hear his voice and he sang and the camel bolted with the guest and threw him so that his face was gashed, and infers that animals love a fine voice and the angry one who raises his voice is like the voice of donkeys. This is the lecturer's story, without a source. (needs_source: the story of the guest, the slave and the camels has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:05:00}
- He says gentleness is not in anything but it adorns it (in sense), and tells that a Bedouin pressed the Prophet on a matter and the Prophet said to him rejoice, and that he said to Abu Musa and Bilal rejoice and they rejoiced, then [[person/umm-salama|Umm Salama]] called from behind the screen to leave something for your mother, and that two men proposed to two daughters, a younger and an elder, and one wanted the younger and the other would accept only the younger so the first yielded gently, and says a fine presentation enters hearts. These are the lecturer's stories in sense, without chains. (needs_source: stories and sayings without a chain or source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:07:30}
- He tells that [[person/abu-hanifa|Abu Hanifa]] supported his pupil Abu Yusuf ([[person/abu-yusuf-al-qadi|Abu Yusuf al-Qadi]]) and gave his mother two dirhams a day so that he might give himself to knowledge, then when Abu Yusuf began teaching in a circle before he was ripe Abu Hanifa sent a pupil to ask him the case of the tailor and the cloth, in which he erred, and when he returned and asked Abu Hanifa he said if he altered the cloth in the agreed manner he deserves his wage and if he seized it he does not, so Abu Yusuf knew that he must keep his place until Abu Hanifa died. He infers that admonition with a gentle excuse lowers no one. This is the lecturer's story, without a chain. (needs_source: the story of Abu Hanifa, Abu Yusuf and the tailor has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:12:00}
- He says we are not infallible in words and gives language examples without building on them, and that one should present well: whoever sold his car says I sold it and the matter ends without troubling people, and he criticises heavy jesting that wounds, such as a wife telling her husband you deserve two slaps, or one who says to the sneezer God's curse. He tells (in sense) that the Prophet persuaded a youth who asked leave for fornication by asking would you like it for your mother, your sister, your paternal aunt and maternal aunt, and he said no, and he said so too people do not like it for their mothers. See [[concept/zina|fornication]]. This is the lecturer's view and story. (needs_source: the report of the youth who asked leave for fornication is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:14:30}
- He tells (in sense) the account of the Prophet's daughter [[person/zaynab-bint-muhammad|Zaynab]] leaving Makkah for Madina, her husband [[person/abu-al-as-ibn-al-rabi|Abu al-As]] sending his brother Kinana to lead her camel, Quraysh intercepting her and Habbar ibn al-Aswad frightening her so she fell and miscarried, then [[person/abu-sufyan-ibn-harb|Abu Sufyan]] told Kinana that her leaving openly after Badr increases our rancour so wait until the people calm and then take her out, which he did days later, handing her to [[person/zayd-ibn-haritha|Zayd ibn Haritha]], and infers that calm and gentleness are more useful than anger. This is the lecturer's account, in sense and without a chain. (needs_source: the account of Zaynab's leaving is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:18:30}
- He gives the example of one who borrowed a book and returned it smelling of food, and of a husband who buys his paper and checks the small change to hide something, and says persuasion for a falsehood by any means grows the tree of resentment that bears only bitterness and gloom so the wide house becomes a narrow tomb, and with a smile and breadth of chest the hut is dearer than the palace, and mentions verses he ascribes to [[person/maysun-bint-bahdal|Maysun bint Bahdal]], mother of [[person/yazid-ibn-muawiya|Yazid ibn Mu'awiya]], preferring the desert tent to the palace, which are not reproduced (a story without a chain). This is the lecturer's view and story. (needs_source: the ascription of the verses to Maysun has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:22:00}
- He advises that when we wish to alert someone to a shortcoming we mention his merits first and not put our hand on the wound if the matter is embarrassing, and tells (in sense) that [[person/umar-ibn-al-khattab|Umar]] adjured whoever had broken wind to rise and make ablution and [[person/abd-al-rahman-ibn-awf|Abd al-Rahman ibn Awf]] said would that you had adjured us all, and that when Aisha struck the dish the Prophet said your mother was jealous, and says life is not a match in which one wins over another, and that we must listen well. See [[person/aisha|Aisha]]. This is the lecturer's view and stories. (needs_source: the stories of Umar and Abd al-Rahman and of Aisha striking the dish are in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:28:30}
- He says the right time, the polite style and brevity are keys to persuasion. Calls: Abu Jarrah asks how to convey the kind word to our children and about lying between spouses; Muhammad from al-Ain says the Prophetic guidance suits every age and that rage not carried out becomes rancour; Fatima from Libya offers condolences for the victims of a Libyan aircraft that fell and asks how to treat hard hearts; Sa'd proposes a channel without advertisements; Mona from Ajman complains of problems with her irritable husband; and Raqiya from Libya, whose husband does not accept advice. Personal details come from text partly garbled. (needs_source: the saying that rage becomes rancour has no source and the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:36:00}
- On conveying the kind word to children he says it is by conduct and not by preaching, so the child does not hear his father's voice loud enough for the neighbours. On lying between spouses he says a house full of lies is one the angels flee and the devils inhabit. To Muhammad he says the Prophetic guidance suits every time and place and the community tired when it left it, citing 'had you been harsh and hard of heart they would have scattered from around you' ([[passage/quran-3-159|Al Imran 159]]). For those with hard hearts he advises remembering death (in sense) and praying for them, and not speaking to the miser much about money but beginning with other things and speaking to him at a moment of serenity. This is the lecturer's answer. (needs_source: the advice of remembering death for the hard-hearted is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:41:00}
- He sees channels without advertisements as a call to owners of wealth and continuing charity to endow them like mosques. To Mona and Raqiya he says not to provoke the husband's irritability, not to come near him at the hour of anger and to pray for his calm. He ends with a prayer for the children in the examinations. This is the lecturer's answer. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep38 00:42:30}

## Episode 39: the school year and safe homes
- He returns to the programme after Ramadan and the feast, and says this is the first day schools open in most Arab and Islamic countries, and prays that it be a year of good for pupils, students, teachers and the educational administrations. This is the lecturer's announcement. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:02:00}
- He tells his sons and daughters the pupils that Adam excelled the angels by knowledge ([[person/adam|Adam]]; [[passage/quran-2-31|al-Baqara 31]]), wishes the inventor of printing had been a Muslim, mentions that the first revelation was 'read' then 'Nun, and the pen' ([[passage/quran-96-1|al-Alaq 1]] and [[passage/quran-68-1|al-Qalam 1]]), and counsels them to learn character first, and that the average student makes up his shortfall with upright conduct, uprightness and prayer and does not lend his ear for hours to songs since they weaken concentration. This is the lecturer's guidance. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:05:00}
- He tells the teachers they are trustees over our children, that their reward is in patience, and that they are heirs of the prophets (in sense), mentions the line of the Prince of Poets in honour of the teacher and says to heed him and not Ibrahim Tuqan in his jesting, without quoting the two verses, calls on parents to stay in touch with school administrations and follow their children, wishes end-of-year examinations were abolished and assessment spread over the year, and sees a complete triangle of ministries of education, teachers, children and parents. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the report that scholars are the heirs of the prophets is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:08:30}
- He describes one who hates knowledge as intellectually abnormal or deficient in mind (his harsh view), says the child comes down with his hands clenched and the dead is washed with his hands open (without a chain), so the world is built by the method of the Qur'an and Sunna, and that modern civilisation is like the Sphinx, a human head and an animal body, and that some religions did not believe in God, and says the word extremism is a Western media word from the late 1970s and the legal term is ghuluw ([[passage/quran-4-171|al-Nisa 171]]), then speaks of worldly extremism: waste, squandering, excessive adornment, clinging to the world and our heedlessness of twenty million displaced by the floods in Pakistan (his figure). This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. (needs_source: the report on the child's clenched hand, the claim that the word is a Western media coinage and the number displaced have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:12:30}
- He says the male is not like the female ([[passage/quran-3-36|Al Imran 36]]) and it is not meant to prefer one, for God has preferred some of them over others ([[passage/quran-4-34|al-Nisa 34]]), and [[person/nuh|Nuh]] and [[person/lut|Lut]] were tried with two disbelieving wives, holds that the girl's subjects of study should differ from the boy's because of the difference in make-up, and mentions medical claims that a man's temperature, blood and nerve links between the centre of emotion and speech differ from a woman's, so the man does not express his feelings easily while in the woman the centres of speech and emotion are one. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. (needs_source: the medical claims on the man's temperature, blood and nerves have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:19:30}
- He describes the burden of the woman teacher with a hundred pupils each with a problem, the limits on beating in schools, and then what is asked of her at home, and says the woman loves warm words, that a survey of twenty thousand men and twenty thousand women found the man better at proposing solutions and the woman stronger at carrying them out (his claim), and that the Arab man counts advice as criticism and the woman counts it as concern, so children do not heed their fathers. He tells that the Prophet would kiss [[person/fatima|Fatima]] between her eyes whenever she came (in sense). This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the survey of twenty thousand and the kissing of Fatima are in sense with no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:23:00}
- He says we are two complementary parties and not quarrelling ones, that intellectual maturity is by religion, that a person is measured by his virtues and not his posts, that the Sunna is a method of action and application of God's Book and not only sitting prayer and glorification after sunset, and that the Companions were a Qur'an walking on two feet. He warns against the child seeing his father proud. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:26:00}
- He says the man sometimes does not value the woman's jealousy and the woman does not understand the man's, and tells (in sense) that when one of the Prophet's wives sent food [[person/aisha|Aisha]] broke the dish and the Prophet said your mother was jealous, and took her dish and sent another. He says life requires mending what we spoil, that apology whitens hearts, and that the best of people are the best to their families (in sense). This is the lecturer's view and story. (needs_source: the account of Aisha breaking the dish and the report 'best to his family' are in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:28:30}
- He says some girls imitate boys and some boys girls and cites the cursing of those who imitate (in sense, without a chain), that the media pours in with no immunity in the children because the father is busy and the mother absent so they become orphans of feeling, that guardianship is not striking nor expelling from the house, that a woman does not leave her home in anger ([[passage/quran-65-1|al-Talaq 1]]), and that one raised in a disturbed house may become a complicated wife, so homes must calm at the start of the school year. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the report of the cursing of those who imitate is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:33:30}
- He hopes a giant generation will arise to restore the glory of [[person/khalid-ibn-al-walid|Khalid ibn al-Walid]], [[person/salah-al-din-yusuf-ibn-ayyub|Saladin]], [[person/abu-hanifa|Abu Hanifa]] and [[person/ibn-taymiyya|Ibn Taymiyya]], and says God put military, social, cultural and civilisational struggle on a level, so the engineer, the doctor, the researcher and the woman teacher all strive to raise the word of God. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:35:00}
- Calls: Abu Jarrah asks advice for teachers on dealing with children and for families on respecting the teacher; a woman teacher asks prayer; Abu Abdullah says the good direction is from the home and the family teaches its children how to treat the teacher; and Abd al-Mun'im proposes turning cafes into squash courts or computer centres and making a mid-week lesson available on the lecturer's site. Personal details come from text partly garbled. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:44:00}
- To the teachers he says they are fathers and mothers and our children are a trust in their hands, that teachers have their problems but with their high resolve they are patient, and tells (in sense) that in the past the boy would hide from his teacher out of respect, and tells a woman teacher who asks prayer that if you want to know your station with God look at what He has stationed you in (a saying without a chain), that the pupils taught at her hands are continuing charity, and that patience is half of faith (in sense). He says father and mother keep the child from the fire of this world and the teacher from the fire of the hereafter (his claim). This is the lecturer's answer. (needs_source: the saying 'if you want to know your station', the report 'patience is half of faith' and the claim about the teacher and the fire of the hereafter have no chain or source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:41:00}
- He says father and mother should earn lawfully so that God puts blessing in their children's knowledge, that no child is born hating learning or reading but comes to hate it because of a family in which no one reads or a curriculum or specialisation imposed on him, so the boy is not forced into a specialisation but left to choose. He calls for a library at home, love of the book and the smell of paper, and mentions a survey ascribed to UNESCO that the European reads thirty-six to forty books a year and that one European's culture equals that of two thousand Arabs (his claim, with garbled figures in the text). This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the UNESCO survey and the reading figures have no source and the text is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:46:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:47:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep39 00:47:30}

## Episode 40: breadth of chest in the home
- He prays for his sons and daughters in the examination season that all be calm and each of us succeed, then puts the question of the episode. This is the lecturer's prayer. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:01:30}
- He asks a question that may seem strange: what will people say after my departure, the stranger and the near, kin and far, and what will the wife say of her husband and the husband of his wife and the children of their father or mother, and says if they say he was like the rain, benefiting wherever he fell, that is a person's happiness, and says he wants to speak of breadth of chest. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:05:00}
- He illustrates with the falcon around which flocks of crows gather and it soars higher so they do not occupy it, says an empty jar makes more noise than a full one and principles make no noise, and tells (in sense) that Aisha described her father [[person/abu-bakr|Abu Bakr]] as weak in body and strong in God's cause, whom storms do not move and who is angry only for a sanctity of God, and that the Prophet did not avenge himself, and says avenging oneself is the way of the owner of the ego who heeds no advice. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: Aisha's description of her father and the account of the Prophet not avenging himself are in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:08:00}
- He says the righteous ask at the grave what he sent ahead while the people of the world ask what he left behind, so it is said he memorised the Qur'an and applied it, opened homes with good and raised his children in obedience, or it is said he left land, a project and a company, and the servant asks himself what will be said of him. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:10:00}
- He says most cases of separation are of two kinds: legal divorce and psychological separation within one house, and what narrows our chest is forgetting the many good things the other side did, for we Arabs are emotional and sometimes rash, and a drop of ink in a large vessel of clear water does not change its colour but one whose chest is tight looks only at it. He mentions that backbiting and tale-bearing were forbidden so that the serenity of chests not be muddied. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:12:30}
- He says when men gather each complains of his wife so she becomes in the others' minds a picture of gloom and ingratitude, and likewise women with their friends, so they charge one another and rebellion is born, and this is tale-bearing ([[passage/quran-68-11|al-Qalam 11]]) whose fruit is tight chests, and before answering one who wronged us we must recall the good that came from him. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:16:00}
- He says we are between justice and favour and need God's favour and not His justice, so we deal with favour that God may deal with us with His favour, and mentions the supplication O God deal with us by Your favour and not by Your justice. He tells that when the Prophet entered Makkah in conquest he said to Quraysh what do you think I will do to you and they said a noble brother, and he said I say only what [[person/yusuf|Yusuf]] said to his brothers, no blame on you today ([[passage/quran-12-92|Yusuf 92]]), and that it was by mercy from God that he was gentle with them ([[passage/quran-3-159|Al Imran 159]]). This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the account of the conquest of Makkah and the saying 'what do you think' are in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:19:00}
- He tells that [[person/abdullah-ibn-abbas|Ibn Abbas]] saw a man and said this one loves me, and when asked how he knew said because I love him, so love is mutual, mentions those who love one another in God in the shade of the Throne, that souls are conscripted soldiers, and that [[person/al-hasan-al-basri|al-Hasan al-Basri]] said to some who claimed brotherhood that a brother puts his hand in his brother's pocket and takes what he wants (the lecturer's stories, in sense, without chains). This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: stories and sayings without a chain or source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:21:30}
- He says one who claims love of God and His Messenger while wronging his family and employees has a false love, that some husbands hurt their wives until they fall ill and some wives are expert in killing their husband's ambition and ridiculing his words, so why do we not encourage one another as birds encourage their chick to fly, and describes some people as fly-natured who settle only on wounds. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:24:00}
- He tells (in sense) that [[person/isa-ibn-maryam|Isa]] passed a stinking dead dog and the disciples said how foul its smell and he said how white its teeth, says be beautiful and you will see existence beautiful, warns against a person seeing all people as bad while he is the first of them, and calls on us to have mercy on the one who errs and to sow good so that God loves us and calls [[person/jibril|Jibril]] that He loves us. This is the lecturer's story and view. (needs_source: the story of Isa and the dog and the report of Jibril's call are in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:26:30}
- Calls: Amina from America asks how one greets someone he is angry with and how to deal with the gap of generations and technology, and ascribes to Ali the saying raise your children for their time; Amira from Saudi Arabia, a second wife who left her work on a condition and whose husband does not support her, asks whether anything is on her because she was married before him by contract only and did not tell him; a woman from Ajman whose husband is in financial distress and whose relatives urge her to leave him; Fatima from Libya asks about the verse on purifying oneself and about the measure of good in a person; Nahid from al-Ain complains of a colleague who harms her despite her kindness; Abd al-Fattah from Dubai comments on breadth of chest and accepting the other; Jawahir tells a dream; Siham asks how to widen her chest with one who hates her; and Abu Khalifa asks whether the verse of the cloaks means the hijab or the niqab. Personal details come from text partly garbled. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:36:30}
- To the woman who asks how to greet one between whom and her there is enmity ([[passage/quran-41-34|Fussilat 34]]) he says to keep on greeting even if not answered, and relates from [[person/abdullah-ibn-masud|Ibn Mas'ud]] his saying would that everyone I greet did not reply since the one who replies is God, so he does not grieve if not answered, and if his visit is refused he does not cut off but leaves a beautiful leaving. On the technology gap he says we must rise to our children's level and not ask them to come down to us. This is the lecturer's answer; the saying of Ibn Mas'ud has no chain. (needs_source: the saying of Ibn Mas'ud has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:39:00}
- To Amira he says the husband should be generous to her in money if he is able and she accepted this condition, and it was obligatory that she tell him she had a prior marriage contract in which he did not consummate, and nothing is on her, God willing. To the woman whose husband is in distress he says abandoning him in the crisis is not of religion nor character, and if her presence burdens him her family may support her temporarily, and leaving the husband in his distress is not a trait of the righteous wife. This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. (needs_source: the ruling that she must tell the husband of a prior contract and the ruling on leaving the husband have no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:41:00}
- To Fatima he says purification is from God ([[passage/quran-53-32|al-Najm 32]]) and that Ibrahim prayed that a messenger be sent among them to purify them ([[passage/quran-2-129|al-Baqara 129]]), and that the measure of good is our nearness to the verses and reports that describe qualities and praise them and condemn others so we measure ourselves by them. To Nahid he says to pray for her colleague in her absence, and if she grieves at her ingratitude then perhaps her deed was not all for God, citing the saying of those who fed for the sake of God we want from you neither reward nor thanks ([[passage/quran-76-9|al-Insan 9]]). To Abd al-Fattah he agrees that breadth of chest is tied to nearness to God and that accepting the other is by putting oneself in his place, and to Siham that she recall what he did for her, even a little good. This is the lecturer's answer. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:44:30}
- To Abu Khalifa on the hijab and niqab he refers to the jurisprudence programme of another scholar and does not answer ([[passage/quran-33-59|al-Ahzab 59]]). He closes with a prayer for those who asked it and calls on us to try this week widening the chest with children, husbands, wives, neighbours, colleagues and the manager, which is a small sapling that bears good. See [[passage/quran-33-59|the verse of the cloaks]]. This is the lecturer's answer. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep40 00:46:30}

## Episode 41: the examination season in the home
- He devotes the episode to the examination season that enters every home, and says we are all examined: the father and mother by responsibility and the children in schools and universities, and divides the responsibility into three axes: the student, then the father, mother and siblings, then the school or university administration with the teachers and markers, and says he will not treat the curricula. He prays that God ease it for the children and soften the hearts of examiners and markers. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:02:30}
- He says the excessive care of some fathers and mothers in the examinations equals the excessive neglect of others: one who goes over with his child every question he missed and counts the marks lost discourages him before the next subject, and the right course is to turn the page, let the child give himself to the coming subject and look hopefully at what is to come. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:04:00}
- He sets against that a father or mother who does not know in which class a son or daughter is and is not concerned whether he passes or fails, cites a line of a poet that the true orphan is the one abandoned by parents or whom they are too busy for, and then equates excessive care with excessive neglect. This is the lecturer's view; the line is not reproduced. (needs_source: the line of verse is given without text or collection) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:05:00}
- He says the tense child should be persuaded that he has studied enough, that the year is over and the teacher has done his part, and be told God will bring the answers to his mind, and that he should begin the question paper with the basmala and seeking refuge and the words our Lord, decide between us and our people in truth ([[passage/quran-7-89|al-A'raf 89]]), then read the questions, start with the easiest, mark what he has answered and move to the harder with God's help without occupying himself with what is going on around him. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:07:00}
- He addresses the invigilators and says a smile calms the children and the examination hall is not a place for terror, that those seated are not thieves but their sons and daughters so they should be treated as he wishes his own to be treated, and holds that the law has a spirit that takes circumstances into account. He says cheating is forbidden for the one who gives and the one who takes, and imagines the cheating engineer building what falls on its residents, the cheating doctor whose operation fails, and the cheating lawyer in cases. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:10:00}
- He says the son needs a father who asks him whether he has revised, advises early sleep after the night prayer, then the dawn prayer and beginning the day with supplication, and says father and mother should tell him they are pleased with him. He mentions from his experience in the mosque over about thirty-five or forty years that boys and girls come in exam days and ask for supplication and then are absent afterwards, and hopes the mosque stays open to them, their fathers and relatives at all times. This is the lecturer's view and experience. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:11:30}
- He says the Arab Muslim mother is emotionally very anxious for her children and often tense, so the father's role is important in reassuring them, that the parents should not belittle a subject or lessen the difficulty of the curricula, and should encourage the son who returns from the examination by saying may it be good, God willing, and that the question he missed is forgotten, giving the boy confidence. He says the soul is made to love one who is good to it, suggests that preachers on Friday guide towards the likely essay topic and how it is written with an introduction, a body and a conclusion, and holds that a father and mother who do not care about their children are selfish. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:14:00}
- He moves to preparing for salvation in the Hereafter, and mentions God's words that among your spouses and children are enemies to you so beware of them ([[passage/quran-64-14|al-Taghabun 14]]), and explains it as that they become so if they are not brought up on the Qur'an, love of God, values, kindness to parents, responsibility and high aspiration. He says [[person/usama-ibn-zayd|Usama ibn Zayd]] led an army that included [[person/abu-bakr|Abu Bakr]] and [[person/umar-ibn-al-khattab|Umar]] when he was seventeen, that [[person/muhammad-ibn-al-qasim|Muhammad ibn al-Qasim]] conquered Sind at sixteen, and that a youth before one of the Abbasid caliphs gave his age as the age of Usama when he led. This is the lecturer's view; the ages are his. (needs_source: the ages and the youth before the caliph are without a chain and the text is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:16:30}
- He says a child's tension on the night before the examination may come from a father who scolds much and is complexed since childhood, and claims that Hitler's stepfather used to lock him in a room so a fear of the narrow place stayed with him until the bunker, and that psychological complexes make a father who hates his children or neglects them. He says the child needs to hear what lifts his morale and not be mocked for his feelings. This is the lecturer's view and claim. (needs_source: the account of Hitler's childhood is without a source and is the lecturer's) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:19:00}
- He criticises the son who stays at thirty-five tied to his mother who is jealous of his wife, and says he must honour her but it is not reasonable that she be angry with her son's wife for wearing something beautiful, and cites a line of a poet on the effect of a lacking mother. He then tells that [[person/hind-bint-utba|Hind]] took her son [[person/muawiya-ibn-abi-sufyan|Mu'awiya]] at six and walked with him before the great men of Quraysh and one said he will lead his people if he grows up, and Hind said she would lose him if he did not lead all the Arabs, and concludes that mothers of high aspiration raise to aspiration. This is the lecturer's view; the story is in sense. (needs_source: the story of Hind and Mu'awiya has no chain and the speaker's identity is garbled, and the verse is not identified) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:21:00}
- He says our children have genius and intelligence and need a father who stirs it, a mother who encourages it and a teacher who holds it and feels the boy is his own son, and that the good a teacher does for the children is not lost with God. He whispers to the invigilators and markers to overlook small mistakes, not to frighten the children, to put them at ease, to give extra time to one who stumbled, and says girls are emotional and may break down in the hall so the invigilator should be a father or mother to her. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:23:00}
- He says these examinations recall the greatest examination on the Day of Resurrection, where there is no committee of favours, no intermediary and no call from a minister, and the humble servant stands before the Majestic Lord, and it is said to him read your record, sufficient is yourself against you this day as accountant ([[passage/quran-17-14|al-Isra 14]]), and mentions the caller calling from a near place ([[passage/quran-50-41|Qaf 41]]) and that the one given his record in his right hand says here, read my record ([[passage/quran-69-19|al-Haqqa 19]]), I expected to meet my reckoning ([[passage/quran-69-20|al-Haqqa 20]]), so he is in a pleasing life ([[passage/quran-69-21|al-Haqqa 21]]), and says in this examination there is no appeal and no supplementary sitting. He describes the call with a phrase about decayed bones without a chain. He says our children are a blessing to be thanked by raising them well. This is the lecturer's view; the call is in sense. (needs_source: the phrase of the call to the decayed bones has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:25:30}
- One caller asks that examinations be abolished entirely so that a person learns for knowledge and servanthood, and the lecturer says the first to rejoice would be the children and then the examiners and markers. A mother wants to learn matters to plant in her little girl when she grows up, and he prays for her. Callers' details are kept general. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:27:30}
- Some callers ask for financial or medical help for a father who lost his sight and a sick son, and he says the programme is religious and educational and requests for help belong to other programmes on the Emirates television channels, and prays for their recovery. Callers' details are kept general and part is garbled. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:31:30}
- He supports the caller's suggestion that advanced schools distribute marks over the student's effort through the whole year so only ten or twenty percent remains for the end, since the boy may be ill or in an unsettled home in the days of the examination, and hopes educators will think about this. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:30:30}
- On a question about an oath in the form 'by the forbidden' he does not answer but refers to the jurisprudence programme on oaths, vows and expiations of another scholar so that specialities do not overlap, and no ruling is given here. This is the lecturer's referral. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:34:00}
- To one who wants verses to reassure her examined daughter he directs to the two surahs of refuge before sleep with blowing into the hands three times and wiping the body, and reading the Fatiha on the way to the hall. To the mother of a six-month girl he says to put character, values and principles first and then knowledge, and to benefit from reading, scholarly gatherings and the Islamic satellite channels and their programmes on bringing up children. This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. (needs_source: the manner of the protective recitation before the examination has no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:35:30}
- To the woman who complains that her husband loves his family and she differs with her mother-in-law he says that the husband's love for his family is necessary and that she should count what she meets from the mother-in-law as raising degrees and expiating sins, bearing it for God and then for the husband so she has credit with him. To the one who asks about his wife's mother whose concern is money he says to be generous to her if money is her concern, that it may come from ignorance or a wish to control her children after their marriage, and we do not want to make enemies because her pleasure brings blessing into the home. This is the lecturer's answer and view. (needs_source: the claim that the husband's love of his family is necessary and that bearing the mother-in-law raises degrees has no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:37:00}
- On teaching languages he says there is no objection to a boy learning several languages, for he can do three, four and five, but not at the expense of the mother tongue, and he does not like members of a Muslim home to speak other than Arabic inside the house, and that study today is in two languages because the West led in knowledge in recent centuries, and that the language of science was Arabic in the first ten centuries. To the student who sees cheating in the hall he says to report to the invigilator or head because cheating wrongs those who did well all year. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:38:30}
- He is asked about the saying of some shaykhs that one who has prayed the witr does not pray the night prayer, and answers that some scholars permit it for one not accustomed to night prayer, and whoever is accustomed ends it with the witr. He thanks a caller for a suggestion that a family adopt programmers who make games that come from our values and religion in place of the PlayStation, and says it is a pleasant suggestion. This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. (needs_source: the answer on witr and night prayer names no scholar and no evidence) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:41:00}
- He closes with a prayer for the children in the exam days for steadfastness, freedom from tension and reliance on God, that the questions be coolness and safety and that they be among the successful and distinguished and a delight of the eyes, and asks viewers to pray.
