This is the lecturer's prayer. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep41 00:45:00}

## Episode 42: the art of listening in the home
- He says the past episodes were a probing of the human soul, and today's subject is why we do not listen to one another, speaking at the same time, with voices in our settings raised, so that some foreign visitors think we are in constant quarrel, and no one waits for the speaker to finish, and some never tire of talking and thousands of words leave the mouth. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:03:30}
- He traces the absence of listening to the roots of pride and arrogance: the proud one wants to hear only the echo of his voice and sees that he alone speaks rightly, and gives the example of the man who looks on his wife as not worth listening to, pleading that he found his fathers on a way ([[passage/quran-43-23|al-Zukhruf 23]]). He mentions in contrast a woman of orderly mind and persuasive manner who looks down from a height on a simple husband and counts his opinions worthless, and says the result is frustration of one side, or each keeping what is inside, or the loss of consultation from the home. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:05:30}
- He says consultation has gone from homes although God commanded His Prophet with consult them in the matter ([[passage/quran-3-159|Al Imran 159]]), and tells (in sense) that [[person/hubab-ibn-al-mundhir|al-Hubab ibn al-Mundhir]] asked the Prophet about the place of encampment at Badr whether it was revelation or opinion, war and counsel and he took his view, that [[person/umm-salama|Umm Salama]] advised him at al-Hudaybiyya and he took her counsel, and that [[person/abu-bakr|Abu Bakr]] and [[person/umar-ibn-al-khattab|Umar]] advised him and he took their counsel. He says the father busy with a football match instead of listening to his wife teaches her not to consult him again, so he is marginalised in her eyes and she may become one of his hard trials. This is the lecturer's view; the accounts have no chains. (needs_source: the accounts of Badr, al-Hudaybiyya and the counsel of the two shaykhs are in sense without chains) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:08:00}
- He says among the calamities of homes is advising a party at a time when advice is not fit: sociologists likened the retreat of a depressed man to his entering a cave and drawing him out at the wrong time to provoking a dragon, and a woman by her emotion wants to bring him out, so the man gives her ten percent of his attention or is angered or his arrogance swells like a tumour and he accepts no advice. He says advice is right only in moments of affection and that advice in public is a scandal, so a son or daughter is not addressed in front of siblings or mother, and cites the saying be a fire and not a blaze. This is the lecturer's view; the sociologists are not named. (needs_source: the likening is ascribed to sociologists without name or source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:12:30}
- He says the wife who presents her family's problem to her husband may find two kinds of man: one who does not like to share because the matter is sensitive, and a positive, reasonable man who advises without whim, and the calamity is that she rejects his advice time after time so he stops intervening. He says a man under pressure becomes more focused seeking a solution while a woman is clouded by her emotion and complains to her friends, mother and sister, and finds her rest only in the breast of a gentle husband. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:15:00}
- He says listening is learned first from the Qur'an in its words listen to it and be silent ([[passage/quran-7-204|al-A'raf 204]]), and the children must see the example in the parents so all fall silent until the smallest child finishes speaking. He says he saw in scholars' books on the life of the Companions a picture of their homes, that no wife complained she had not gone out for three days, that the Prophet raced [[person/aisha|Aisha]], and that one who runs with his wife on the pavement today is accused by his friend of weak manhood, and the woman who never goes out finds the house a detention. He criticises the many questions where were you and why were you late because they desertify emotion and turn the home into a policeman and a thief. This is the lecturer's view; the accounts have no chains. (needs_source: the account of the Prophet racing Aisha and the accounts of the Companions' homes have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:19:00}
- He mentions the solemn covenant between spouses ([[passage/quran-4-21|al-Nisa 21]]) and says it must be observed and not shaken, and adds that the wife who sees her husband not listening may feel she has no credit in his heart when it is not so, and Satan gives her twenty reasons to think he does not love her. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:20:30}
- He says some homes are built on the idea of gain and loss as if a market or a stock exchange, while marriage is the highest institution in which each party should soften to the other, and mentions the description of the believers humble towards the believers, criticises the many demands on one who proposes, and brothers who prevent their widowed or divorced sisters from marrying. He says giving is by favour and not by reckoning, that marriage turned into a battle is a way for each to bring the other into the Fire ([[passage/quran-37-22|al-Saffat 22]]), and prays that spouses dwell in Paradise ([[passage/quran-2-35|al-Baqara 35]]) by fewer problems and obedience to God. He mentions a story of a woman advising her daughter on her wedding night, without naming her or a chain. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. (needs_source: the story of the mother's advice on the wedding night is unnamed, without a chain, and the text is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:24:30}
- He advises wives to be calm when the husband is angry and not to argue or advise in that state, like one who lost a deal and is returning home and she lists his faults, comparing it to a man who sees a fire and calls the fire brigade before asking its cause. He mentions that [[person/khadija|Khadija]] said to the Prophet after the first revelation that you bear the burdens and help on the misfortunes of truth and God will never disgrace you, and that she stood by her husband in the crisis, and prays that wives and husbands complete one another and each widen the other's chest. This is the lecturer's view; the account of Khadija is in sense. (needs_source: the account of Khadija after the first revelation is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:27:30}
- He says some spouses want to give the least in order to take the most, which is a commercial idea and not a marital one, and mentions God's words we feed you only for the sake of God, we want from you neither reward nor thanks ([[passage/quran-76-9|al-Insan 9]]), and tells (in sense) that one who takes back a gift is like a dog returning to its vomit, so one who gave his wife a gift and then asks her to return it has erred. He says giving without limit expects reward only from God, and that one who gives and then reproaches when he finds no return begins enmity, for reproach is the start of enmity. This is the lecturer's view; the hadith is in sense. (needs_source: the hadith on taking back a gift is in sense without a reference) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:30:00}
- Among the questions: on whom marital disputes fall, and the caller comments that Arabs do not accept being told you are the one at fault, and on the foundations of bringing up children. A caller asks how one knows whether a trial raises degrees or is a punishment, and cites the words whoever turns away from My remembrance, for him is a straitened life ([[passage/quran-20-124|Ta Ha 124]]). Callers' details are kept general and parts of the text are garbled. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:32:30}
- He answers the one who asked how to know a trial is a raising of degrees or a punishment: one who is on God's path, doing what good he can, tied to God's book and loving scholars, then meets illness or worry, it is a raising of degrees and expiation of sins, and one far from God and deviant in his affairs who meets a trial, it is from His words whoever turns away from My remembrance ([[passage/quran-20-124|Ta Ha 124]]). This is the lecturer's answer. (needs_source: the judgement on the kind of trial by the state of the person has no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:35:00}
- To the woman who asks whether a wife may give charity from her husband's money without his knowledge though he allows it, he says it is good and blessing so long as he does not object and she helps him to have the money in the scale of his good deeds, and to the one who complains of laziness in the night prayer he advises much supplication in prostration that God make her one who keeps the prayers in their times. This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. (needs_source: the ruling on a wife's charity from her husband's money has no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:43:30}
- To a caller who loves a man who turned out to be married, aged twenty-seven or twenty-nine and afraid of spinsterhood, he says the information came in stages and hopes God grants her a complete husband and not half a husband. To a husband whose wife reproaches him that she does housework he says reproach does no good and that a kind word is better than charity followed by injury ([[passage/quran-2-263|al-Baqara 263]]) and we should not harm one another with such words. This is the lecturer's answer; details kept general. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:46:30}
- On a question about a divorced wife attending the funeral of her former husband and his will that she should not attend, he says in a garbled text that the foreigner should have attended anyway and the matter is wrong from its beginning. On buying a house with a bank loan he refers to the fatwa programme of another scholar on the Dubai channel and does not answer so that specialities do not overlap. This is the lecturer's referral; no ruling is given here. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:47:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:47:30}
- To one wrongly accused while innocent he says to use a person whom the accuser loves and listens to well, because we listen only to those we love. To a caller whose sister wants the hijab and whose husband refuses and threatens divorce he says to bring virtuous people the husband respects to speak with him, and that the hijab is a command of God like prayer and zakat. This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. (needs_source: the ruling on the hijab compared to prayer has no evidence stated and the text is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:47:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:48:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:48:30}
- To one about to marry he says to choose well, putting religion first, not to take the secrets of the home out to the wife's or husband's family and to keep the problems inside the home, and to the one asking on whom the dispute between spouses falls he says the husband may err and the wife may err but steer right and draw near. On bringing up children he says lawful earnings first, then practical example, then advice and supplication, our Lord, grant us from our spouses and children the delight of our eyes ([[passage/quran-25-74|al-Furqan 74]]). This is the lecturer's answer. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:43:00}
- On a caller's question that many listen and do not carry out, he says the listener should listen not with his ear but with his heart, mentions the words lend me the hearing of your hearts, and describes one who hears with one ear and lets it out of the other as having a hidden pride, and prays for their guidance. This is the lecturer's answer; the saying has no chain. (needs_source: the saying 'lend me the hearing of your hearts' has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:45:00}
- He says the next episode will be on the art of fairness between husband and wife and between boys and girls in the home, and closes with a prayer that God secure homes by obedience to Him and prepare righteous husbands for the daughters and righteous wives for the sons. This is the lecturer's prayer. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:48:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep42 00:49:00}

## Episode 43: the art of giving in the home
- He says giving is an art many do not master, and people stand between excess and neglect, between the generosity of [[person/hatim-al-tai|Hatim al-Ta'i]] and the miserliness of Madir, and that one who gives to his children and wife beyond what he can bear may be met by a generous or a base nature, the generous being like good soil that grows the giving and the sandy land holding no water and growing nothing, and yet this does not stop the people of giving from giving by kind word and kind dealing. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the proverbial pair Hatim and Madir and the image of the land have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:03:00}
- He links excessive pampering to luxury and mentions God's words when We want to destroy a town We command its affluent and they transgress in it ([[passage/quran-17-16|al-Isra 16]]), and says Islam does not forbid the beautiful in clothes and dwellings, that the blameworthy affluent is one who forgot his share of the Hereafter and remembered the blessing and forgot the Giver, that praiseworthy ease is that God see the trace of His blessing on you, and blameworthy that the rich withhold money, the man of standing his standing, and the man of knowledge his knowledge. This is the lecturer's view and interpretation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:05:30}
- He says our children in this age are not content with little nor satisfied with much and have a culture of grumbling: I wear like the children of so and so, I ride such a car, and this culture bears boredom, then weariness, then slamming doors in the faces of the parents and fretting at siblings, and so the calamity of disobedience to parents begins, for they feel the parents' service is owed them. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:07:00}
- He says one of the afflictions of the culture of luxury is that many girls at twelve or fifteen wish to be males, and traces it to their femininity being denied by those around them: the father finds the fourth daughter a burden, the brother returns at night and no one blames him while she is blamed if she is late at sunset, so she feels lesser. He says he does not call for the girl to stay out late like her brother, but for looking at what the family spends and gives to each, that obedience to parents is owed by the children, and that taking such cases to psychiatrists does not treat the root. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation; no count is given. (needs_source: the claim that the phenomenon is widespread and its causes have no count or source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:10:30}
- He says part of the culture of luxury is that a possessive greed arises in children: the boy wants to possess everything in the house and the girl all the parents' attention, and when grown they seek all the posts and may cheat and compete without honour, that ingratitude for livelihood is the denial of the truth ([[passage/quran-28-58|al-Qasas 58]]), and that some children look with contempt at one whose father is an official of lower grade. He holds that fathers should teach their children to look at those below them in worldly matters, one who cannot afford a ticket, one who is disabled, an orphan, or one who lost his mother, and says divorce rates rise greatly in summer (his claim). This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. (needs_source: the summer rise in divorce has no count or source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:14:30}
- He says human wants are endless like one who drinks sea water, which only increases his thirst, that makers and advertisers aim at what is in the pockets, that children today do not rejoice at a gift because they have everything and the gift became relative so they compare their father's gift with their uncle's, and that fathers want from their children only contentment whereas we have come to court our children that they be pleased with us, which is humiliation. He says the successful think of the solution and the failed of the problem and the excuses of the failed never run dry. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:19:30}
- He cites the saying do not think of what is lost lest you lose what is present, and says to the son thank your Lord that you have a home and a family, for there are those who live in camps, under bombardment and hunger, and none of us is nobler than they, the better being with God. He says one who plants luxury reaps possessive greed and becomes a manager claiming expertise in legal, financial and engineering affairs alike. He tells (in sense) that the Prophet said to [[person/aisha|Aisha]] be good neighbour to God's favours, for no people to whom God gave favour did not treat it well but it left them. This is the lecturer's view; the saying and the hadith have no chain. (needs_source: the saying on not thinking of the lost and the hadith on being a good neighbour to God's favours have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:23:00}
- He criticises the father who is content that his daughter go out unveiled while he prays and claims religion, says the hijab is a command of God, compares him to one who sees fire on his daughter's clothes and puts it out, so how does he not guard against the fire of the Hereafter, and criticises a father taking his daughters to a hairdresser to look beautiful before people, saying manliness and protective jealousy have gone and that this is the giving of devils and not the giving of God, the Qur'an and the Sunna, so it is plunder and not giving. This is the lecturer's view and judgement; the passage is partly garbled. (needs_source: the ruling on the hijab and the comparison have no evidence stated and the text is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:26:00}
- He cites the saying of [[person/ibn-ata-allah-al-iskandari|Ibn Ata Allah]] if you are given understanding in withholding, withholding becomes the very giving, and says that one granted daughters and no son has a withholding that is giving, mentioning His words He grants females to whom He wills and grants males to whom He wills ([[passage/quran-42-49|al-Shura 49]]) or gives them both males and females and makes whom He wills barren ([[passage/quran-42-50|al-Shura 50]]), and that a son may be an enemy ([[passage/quran-64-14|al-Taghabun 14]]). He then says immunising children against the germs of globalisation is by lawful money, fear of God and the care of the parents, and gives the parable of the people of the ship who cast lots: if they leave the one who would pierce his share all perish together. This is the lecturer's view; the hadith is in sense without a chain. (needs_source: the saying of Ibn Ata Allah has no place named and the hadith of the ship is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:28:00}
- He says nothing is impossible for the believer, rejects naming an illness malignant for the malignant is one who leaves the lawful for the unlawful, and mentions the supplication on entering the toilet, and says medicine fails so far to cure some diseases but nothing is impossible. He says the father who answers his son in all he asks spoils him, and that we should say yes in three things and no in four on condition that the refusal is reasoned and for his good, because the boy's thought is limited. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation; no medical claim is made here. (needs_source: his remark on the 'malignant' illness and medicine's ability has no medical source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:30:30}
- He says he wants to reorder the priorities of the Muslim home: had we married the bridegroom and bride on God's book and the Sunna of His Prophet they would live by them, but we live on my mother said and my father said and my aunt's daughter married thus. To a caller whose work resembles judging between people and who fears wronging he says his worry is a sign of health, and that one who strives to hear the sides and does not judge by whim, God puts the truth on his tongue. He agrees with a caller that the intrusion of third parties into the couple's life causes problems. This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. (needs_source: the remark that God puts truth on the tongue of the striving judge has no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:34:30}
- To the woman who asks about money that came to her from usury, aged thirty-two, and fears getting rid of it all since it is all she has, he says, and God knows best, that she does not rid herself of it at once if she has no other income, but purifies it step by step with lawful money from work, like a cup full of mud and sand with a clean tap opened on it until it is driven out. He refers the question in principle to the specialised fatwa programmes. This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. (needs_source: the method of purifying usurious money gradually has no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:36:00}
- To the mother of a girl of three who cries until she gets what she wants he says not to give her each time she cries, and that crying widens the chest and tightens the vocal cords (his claim). To the caller whose father gave her no tenderness and has died he says to pray for him and keep up ties with his paternal uncles and aunts, and tenderness may be inward in many Arab men. To one who complains that those she gives to do not thank he says the child learns thanks from his parents and thanks is awaited from God ([[passage/quran-76-9|al-Insan 9]]). To the mother thinking of work with two small children he says staying with them is a great work and that for thirty years he has called for the state to pay a housewife a salary. This is the lecturer's answer and claim; details kept general. (needs_source: the claim of crying's benefit to the chest and vocal cords has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:43:00}
- To a mother of daughters who forbids them everything they see at their friends' and to a caller who asks whether children's discontent comes from openness, he says openness has an effect, that we should teach children contentment and sufficiency, that giving is by measure and by stages and not every year, and that the child should look in worldly matters at one lower than himself and in religion at one better: one who has memorised ten parts, one who prays the five times in the mosque, one who reads five books. And that the Lord is the first Rearer. This is the lecturer's answer; the saying is in sense without a chain. (needs_source: the rule of looking to the lower in worldly matters and the better in religion is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:46:00}
- He closes that we should understand the art and culture of giving, without excess or neglect, neither the generosity of Hatim who is told to have nearly slaughtered his son for guests, nor the miserliness of Madir who is told to have refused to lend a needle to Yaqub to sew the shirt of [[person/yusuf|Yusuf]]. He prays that God secure homes and provide daughters with righteous husbands and sons with righteous wives. This is the lecturer's prayer; the two stories have no chain. (needs_source: the story of Hatim and the guests and of Madir and the needle have no chain and the text is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:46:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:47:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:47:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep43 00:48:00}

## Episode 44: the affliction of hearsay in the home
- He says homes are secrets, and the more they keep their secrets the stronger their walls and the lasting the affection among their members, and when secrets go out and others put their noses in, homes crack, likening it to the foundation of a building whose materials, if not in right proportion, crack the house with any tremor. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:02:00}
- He says the engagement is a secret and not a party, and that the Prophet commanded us to announce the marriage and keep the engagement hidden, since the betrothed remains a stranger, so if the sides do not agree the engagement is dissolved without harm beyond the dissolving itself, but if the near and far know, the next suitor looks for her faults and why it was broken. He says in the Arab setting we have a scarcity of manliness and an abundance of maleness, and that the suitor's family may speak what harms. This is the lecturer's view; the hadith is in sense without a chain. (needs_source: the hadith on announcing the marriage and hiding the engagement is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:05:30}
- He mentions (in sense) that God dislikes for us qil wa qal (hearsay), waste of wealth and much asking, explains that the word expression derives from crossing, carried by the tongue between two lips like two doors, and that a word kept in the mouth is ours but once out it owns us. He gives the example of a wife who provokes her husband until she says divorce me so that Iblis has used the chance, and of a bad word in the engagement gathering that stays in the suitor's memory thirty years, and that we recall faults strongly and forget good deeds. This is the lecturer's view; the hadith is in sense without a chain. (needs_source: the hadith on the dislike of qil wa qal is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:09:30}
- He says backbiting and tale-bearing are the gravest part of hearsay, for the husband goes to his mother and recites many faults of his wife and forgets twenty good qualities, and had he carried the good ones his mother would have loved him and his wife, and his mother and father are part of him so they take a bad picture of his wife and her family, and the reverse likewise. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:11:30}
- On delayed childbearing he says he likes the medical check before marriage and the psychological check and a certificate from a scholar that the two have studied family jurisprudence, and holds that the husband's family blame the wife as if it were in her hand while it is a provision from God who grants females to whom He wills and males to whom He wills or gives both and makes whom He wills barren ([[passage/quran-42-49|al-Shura 49]], [[passage/quran-42-50|al-Shura 50]]), and that barrenness may be a favour since a child may be disobedient, and mentions the story of [[person/al-khidr|al-Khidr]] with [[person/musa|Musa]] and the killing of the boy ([[passage/quran-18-74|al-Kahf 74]]). This is the lecturer's view and interpretation. (needs_source: the certificate from a scholar on family jurisprudence is the lecturer's suggestion with no evidence) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:14:00}
- He says the intrusion of both spouses' families into the causes of delayed childbearing is a deep wound, that the husband is of full capacity and so is the wife, so there is no need to intervene unless counsel is sought or a specialist doctor, and that homes have secrets. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:17:00}
- He tells (in sense) that the Prophet entered on [[person/fatima|Fatima]] and asked about [[person/ali-ibn-abi-talib|Ali]] and she said he was angry and went out, so he did not ask her the reason but went and found him asleep in the mosque with dust on his shoulder, brushed it off and said get up, Abu Turab, and brought him in to Fatima, that the Prophet saw Fatima grinding with Ali while pregnant and asked Ali's leave to take her turn, and that Fatima told Ali she was dearer to the Prophet than he, so Ali asked the Prophet and he said in sense she was dearer to him and Ali nearer to his heart. He concludes that the Prophet did not put his nose into the secrets of the home but pleased both sides. This is the lecturer's view; the accounts have no chains. (needs_source: the accounts of Fatima and Ali, the grinding and the Prophet's words are in sense without chains) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:20:30}
- He speaks of plural marriage and says the injustice done by many men distorted the image of what God permitted, that each home should be left to its circumstances and we should not put our noses in others' secrets or be meddlesome ([[passage/quran-17-36|al-Isra 36]]), and that a wife whose husband married another should not give away her good deeds by backbiting another wife she does not know, and tells (in sense) that in his illness the Prophet was carried to the house of the one whose turn it was and that he drew lots among his wives. This is the lecturer's view; the account has no chain. (needs_source: the account of the Prophet carried to the house of the one whose turn it was and the lots is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:22:30}
- He says what is between spouses no one knows but the two, and that brothers' wives may carry to a brother what stirs strife between the brothers, and mentions that a Muslim may not shun his brother beyond three days (in sense), that the oath of abstention in the pre-Islamic era had no limit and Islam made it four months ([[passage/quran-2-226|al-Baqara 226]]), and holds that spouses one of whom shuns the other for three days should speak on the fourth. This is the lecturer's view; the hadith is in sense without a chain. (needs_source: the hadith on shunning beyond three days is in sense without a chain and the scholars' saying about the stopping of good deeds is not named) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:26:30}
- He tells (in sense) that [[person/aisha|Aisha]] gestured that [[person/safiyya-bint-huyayy|Safiyya]] was short and the Prophet said she had said a word which, if mixed with the sea water, would change it, and says we mention people's faults such as shortness and dark colour and the like, that the tongue leads either to good or to evil, that one backbiting is worse with God than thirty-six acts of adultery (he ascribes it to scholars), that the Prophet called the second wife a companion and a sister while we call her harm, and that the wives' empty time in the homes called them to tale-bearing. This is the lecturer's view; the accounts have no chains. (needs_source: the account of Aisha and Safiyya and the ascription to scholars on backbiting have no chain and no names) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:29:00}
- He says the mother and the sister of the husband may be counted an unwelcome guest, recalls the rule as you judge you will be judged, defines tale-bearing as carrying news to harm another, and advises the husband and wife, when a tale-bearer comes to them, to tell him that between us there was good and you came to obstruct it, that one who bears tales to you will bear them against you tomorrow, and that a trustworthy person does not carry what was said about him in a moment of anger, and that if everyone said this to every tale-bearer tale-bearing would vanish. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:32:00}
- He tells (in sense) that [[person/umar-ibn-al-khattab|Umar]] offered his daughter [[person/hafsa|Hafsa]] after her widowhood to [[person/abu-bakr|Abu Bakr]] who was silent and to [[person/uthman-ibn-affan|Uthman]] who declined, so he complained of them to the Prophet who said she would marry one better than Abu Bakr and better than Uthman, and the Prophet married her, then Abu Bakr said to him perhaps you were upset when I was silent, for I had heard the Prophet mention her and I was not one to disclose the Messenger's secret. He concludes keeping a secret. This is the lecturer's view; the account has no chain. (needs_source: the account of Umar's offer of Hafsa and Abu Bakr's answer is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:34:30}
- A caller from Germany asks about the dress of a Muslim married to a non-Muslim woman and about marriage without a guardian in Europe, and he says a Muslim married to a non-Muslim should be a good example so that she may accept Islam, that he warns his own children against such marriage, refers the jurisprudential matter to the European Council for Fatwa in Dublin, and says her guardian may be the imam of the mosque or the elder of the community. On the difference between contentment and lack of ambition he says ambition has no limit and contentment is to thank God for what came and accept His decree if it did not. This is the lecturer's answer and referral, not a fatwa. (needs_source: the ruling on the guardian in Europe and on marrying a non-Muslim woman has no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:44:30}
- Callers ask: a pregnant woman suffering with her husband and another who wants a judicial separation because of an illness that passed to her, and he tells her to go to the court and this is God's decree, and to the pregnant woman whose husband comes late he says to be patient and seek reward, perhaps the child will draw its father home. To one who asks about creams containing alcohol he says we do not use them because alternatives are many. To the one who asks about being content with two children he says (in sense) marry and multiply for I will boast of you before the nations, and says perhaps the third will be like Salah al-Din and the fourth like Khalid ibn al-Walid. This is the lecturer's answer; details kept general; not a fatwa. (needs_source: the hadith 'marry and multiply' is in sense without a chain and the rulings on khul' and alcohol creams have no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:46:30}
- To one who sees a channel that incites against Islam he says to cancel it in his home and be content with the good channels. To a caller in Belgium who married a man of another nationality without her family's knowledge and whose family refused he says the family has erred and the husband erred in delaying telling his own family, but it is a lawful, legitimate marriage, and the parents will be persuaded with the days. This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. (needs_source: the ruling on the validity of marriage without the family's knowledge has no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:47:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:47:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:48:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:48:30}
- He gives the viewers the week's duty: to hold their tongues from hearsay and prevent others from carrying news to them, and closes with a prayer that tongues be busy with the remembrance of God and homes be secure and at ease, far from hearsay. This is the lecturer's prayer. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:48:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep44 00:49:00}

## Episode 45: gentleness in the home
- He says everyone complains of noise and violence inside the family and outside it and that gentleness has evaporated, that each is ready to attack and look for the other's faults, that the most important human energy is managing one's feelings and this is managed only by gentleness, and that the violent and tense man is harsh when he should be affectionate and stern when he should be forbearing. He mentions the words of one of the two daughters of the righteous man about [[person/musa|Musa]], the best you can hire is the strong, the trustworthy ([[passage/quran-28-26|al-Qasas 26]]). This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:03:00}
- He says violence in homes begins with the child before the screen, that prison is the school of crime and the small screen the preparatory school of juvenile delinquency, and cites a scientific study he does not name that holds that the electronic game called the PlayStation produces nervous conditions in the brain, and imagines his great-grandfather being invited to the station of play. This is the lecturer's view; the study is not named. (needs_source: the scientific study on the effect of electronic games is unnamed and unsourced) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:06:30}
- He relays numbers from researchers he does not name about a hundred Arab satellite channels monitored for a week: a large count of killings, physical violence, nudity and scenes of wine within eight hours and over a year, and says repetition makes them normal in the child's eyes. He relays percentages he says come from the Saudi Ministry of Interior, the National Centre for Social Research in Egypt and an Algerian government report on violence against children and wives. The numbers in the text are garbled, so they are not recorded. This is the lecturer's statement; the figures are not written down. (needs_source: the statistics ascribed to researchers and institutions have no verified source and the numeric text is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:11:00}
- He says children sometimes lack the tenderness of father and mother so cruelty is fixed in their minds, that they are left without conversation among the adults, mentions the saying of [[person/ali-ibn-abi-talib|Ali]] raise your children for their time and not yours, that some mothers curse the boy as stupid and a devil so he becomes rebellious, and advises that the child be first described by his good character and then given veiled advice, since constant blame in childhood makes him blame his wife in adulthood. He advises gentle reproof sitting beside the child. This is the lecturer's view; the saying ascribed to Ali has no chain. (needs_source: the saying ascribed to Ali has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:16:00}
- He says the very quiet child may need to be shown to a doctor, that the punishment, according to specialists he mentions, is to sit the child alone on a chair one minute for each year of his age and to deprive him of some privileges but not permanently, that early warning and prevention are better than cure, and that the boy spends eight or nine hours at school and we do not know what he stores there. This is the lecturer's view; the specialists are not named. (needs_source: the timed sitting rule is ascribed to unnamed specialists) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:18:30}
- He says children differ like the fingers of the hand so each is treated as suits him, that the violent boy is embraced and the knot in him sought, from cartoon serials or the father's violence to the mother or the mother's domination, and relays from a study in a European country he says is Spain that thirty-nine percent of delinquent youths learned their delinquency from science-fiction and action films (his claim), and calls on directors and media people to fear God in our children and on all of us to be a work team: the father, mother, school, ministry of education, scholars, preachers and media people. This is the lecturer's view; the study has no source. (needs_source: the thirty-nine percent attributed to a European study has no name or source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:22:00}
- He says some men do not pray or pay the zakat on their wealth yet beat their wives citing the Qur'an without knowing the basis of the matter, that some Arabs think visiting a psychiatrist means madness, and that many divorce cases that come to him in recent years are between spouses married less than three years. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation; no count is given. (needs_source: the claim of many divorces before three years has no count) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:23:30}
- He proposes as the solution Shari'a preparation and a culture of dialogue, criticises satellite programmes where each raises his voice so no one's words are clear, and says we must teach our children that all fall silent when a family member speaks and that each has a right to present his view calmly, not as said I show you only what I see ([[passage/quran-40-29|Ghafir 29]]). He says homes should not be like the spider's house whose secrets are exposed ([[passage/quran-29-41|al-Ankabut 41]]), that gentleness enters nothing but adorns it and violence enters nothing but disfigures it (in sense), and that you can know a gentle home by the low voices of its people. This is the lecturer's view; the hadith is in sense without a chain. (needs_source: the hadith on gentleness entering nothing but adorning it is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:27:30}
- Among the questions: on the soundness of a hadith praising one light of means with many dependants (asked and not answered), on children's rights laws in the West if the child complains of his father, on the effect of Arab films on children where directors say they only carry reality, on a hot-tempered husband who swears divorce and is unfaithful, on a divorced woman with five children whose father does not support them, on frequent divorce among the educated before years pass, on bringing up a child in a violent environment, and on the harms of television and games. Callers' details are kept general and parts of the text are garbled. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:34:30}
- To one who fears children's rights laws in the West he says we are charged with strengthening the faith immune system of our children, not killing the germs around us, by supplication, gentleness, fear of God and love. To the caller on a violent environment he says the foetus in the womb is affected by the violence of the parents and the home in which the Qur'an is read, prayer is made and God is remembered brings up a gentle, calm child, and to the one who asks how to protect her children from the media he suggests a clear schedule in which the boy sits with the games half an hour or an hour a week and not seven hours a day. To the one who blames Arab directors he says our reality is the reality of Islam and values, and calls on them to fear God lest it hang on their necks on the Day of Resurrection. This is the lecturer's answer; the claim that the foetus is affected has no source. (needs_source: the claim that the foetus is affected by the parents' violence and the hours of play have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:45:30}
- To the woman who complains of a hot-tempered husband who swears by divorce and is unfaithful he cites the words and We set his wife right for him ([[passage/quran-21-90|al-Anbiya 90]]) and calls to piety and mending what is between us and God that God mend what is between us and people. To the divorced woman with five children he says her son of twenty-three can bear responsibility, that the children have rights on their father and she has the right of custody. To one who asks about people refusing the intervention of advisers he says good intentions are accepted, and mentions sending an arbiter from his family and one from hers ([[passage/quran-4-35|al-Nisa 35]]). This is the lecturer's answer, not a fatwa. (needs_source: the attribution of custody and maintenance rulings has no evidence stated) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:43:30}
- To the one who asks how a girl prepares herself to raise the future generation he says to choose well, not only the handsome but one whose character and religion are pleasing, and tells (in sense) that the Prophet said if one comes to you whose religion and character you like then marry him, or there will be strife in the land and great corruption, and that one who marries a woman for her wealth, beauty, lineage and religion should win the one with religion, and that religion does not mean she is veiled or niqab-wearing but that she is not racist, not extremely jealous and not reproachful, and to marry for God, for whoever marries has secured half his religion, and that the husband's money be lawful, and he defers the story of Abu Hanifa's parents' marriage to a coming episode. He says the husband should not put his wife under the microscope nor mock her, and the episode closes with two rak'as and reducing television viewing. This is the lecturer's answer; the hadiths are in sense without chains. (needs_source: the hadiths on choosing a spouse, the one with religion and half of religion are in sense without chains) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:46:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:47:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:47:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:48:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:48:30}
- He closes that gentleness be the title of our homes, written on them in condition and not in letters, these are homes of gentleness, and prays that God make us among the people of gentleness. This is the lecturer's prayer. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:49:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep45 00:49:30}

## Episode 46: the complementarity of the spouses
- He says the recent episodes are a probing of the human soul, that one who does not study it tires with himself and with those around him, and that when God created from the first soul its mate and spread from them many men and women ([[passage/quran-4-1|al-Nisa 1]]) He made them to complete one another and not to oppose, and that the man wants the woman reasonable and orderly and the woman wants him emotional and romantic, each a demand that is sometimes hard because each has his own nature. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:03:00}
- He presents differences he sees between man and woman: the man distinguishes pity from sympathy and the woman hastens to give, the man counts advice as mockery and the woman accepts it from one she loves, the man looks at life as a hierarchy of struggle and the woman horizontally as friendships and relations, the man when worried withdraws and the woman when worried needs to express herself and be heard, his sight is frontal and hers lateral, and she is more sacrificing and accepts an imperfect husband while the man thinks of another if his wife falls ill, and her feeling is born strong so she weeps at a drop that breaks the back of the cup. This is the lecturer's view and generalisation; no study is named. (needs_source: the claims of psychological differences between man and woman have no study or source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:08:30}
- He says the woman is extreme in feeling, and this is a description and not a blame, giving the example of a mother who weeps with joy and sorrow at her daughter's marriage, divorce and childbirth, and of a mother whose eyelid stays awake over a sick child while the father sleeps, and says the heart's diseases in the man are pride and arrogance and in the woman love of show and care for appearance before guests, that the man is jealous only if he loves and the woman is jealous whether she loves or not, that the woman loves the place with her memories and the man the homeland, and that seven men speak as many words as one woman (his saying). This is the lecturer's view and generalisation; the descriptions have no study. (needs_source: the descriptions and ratios ascribed to woman and man have no study) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:13:30}
- He relays numbers about differences in bodily make-up that say the man is stronger by about thirty percent and his capacity for work is greater because his red blood cells, liver, muscles and lungs are larger, and says this is the dye of God and the fault in homes is that we have not understood they are complementary and not opposed. This is the lecturer's statement; the numbers are not written down. (needs_source: the anatomical and physiological numbers have no scientific source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:15:00}
- He says the man wants from his wife trust, admiration, praise and encouragement and not to be shown weak before people, and the woman wants attention, care, a kind word, thanks and respect, and that respect is a sapling planted in the soil of marriage that bears trust. He claims that the man's emotion is in the right half of the brain and speech in the left so he does not express his feelings while both are on one side in the woman (his claim), and that half of Muslim wives suffer headaches from not being understood (his claim). This is the lecturer's view; the claims have no source. (needs_source: the claim on emotion and speech in the brain halves and the share of wives with headaches has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:19:00}
- He says the man's mental maturity comes late near forty, that the man may seem dull of feeling because he does not express himself, so our grandmothers saw their husbands' love in their deeds and not their words. He tells (in sense) that the Prophet used to put down his knee so that [[person/safiyya-bint-huyayy|Safiyya]] might mount the camel, and that he drank from the place where [[person/aisha|Aisha]] had drunk, and counts this practical love and the romance and taste of Islam. This is the lecturer's view; the accounts have no chains. (needs_source: the accounts of the knee and the Prophet drinking where Aisha drank are in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:22:00}
- He advises the wife not to argue with her husband while he is agitated but to wait until the storm calms, likening it to a dentist who gives an antibiotic before extraction and to the victim of an accident whose bleeding is stopped before the wound is sewn, and says the husband, when he sees his wife in revolt and criticism, should absorb her anger with a kind word and listening, agree to her sound words and command what is right in a right way. He says the girl is taught other than the boy is taught, the delicate one entering literature and Shari'a and the boy engineering, not out of deficiency in her but that she be put where her nature fits. This is the lecturer's view, not a fatwa. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:26:30}
- He relays figures from a statistic he says is British from about 2007 or 2008 that women are fewer than men in drug trafficking, violence, theft and smuggling, and infers that the woman reckons matters despite the primacy of her emotion. This is his statement; the statistic has no source and the figures are not written down. (needs_source: the British statistic is ascribed without a source and the figures are garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:27:30}
- Among the questions: a caller's remark that the oriental man looks down on his wife before her family, the reasons for plural marriage and the misyar marriage, a wife who changed through her religiosity so her husband is vexed with her, a question on the wife's prayer for her husband's mother and whether the hadith your mother then your mother is for the husband alone, a girl whose marriage contract was made with a man who lies about his whereabouts, and the debtor's supplication. Callers' details are kept general and parts of the text are garbled. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:37:00}
- To the debtor he says to pray sincerely, mentioning the words who answers the distressed when he calls on Him ([[passage/quran-27-62|al-Naml 62]]), to pray with the Prophet's supplication on worry, grief, debt and the mastery of men (in sense) and to seek forgiveness much. To the woman who became religious and whose husband is vexed with her he says to show the fruit of religiosity in good conduct as a wife and gentleness. To the one who asks about the hadith your mother then your mother he says it applies to the male because a woman's husband may prevent her from kindness to her mother. To the one who made a contract with a liar he says a house built on lies is built on sand, to wait until he is treated of his lying and not to marry a liar. On plural marriage and misyar he refers to the jurisprudence programme of another scholar and does not answer. This is the lecturer's answer and referral, not a fatwa; the hadith and supplication are in sense without a chain. (needs_source: the hadith and supplication are in sense without a chain and the restriction of the hadith to the male has no evidence) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:41:30}
- To one who told him he speaks theoretically of an ideal society that has died out he says that realistic ideality was realised at the hands of the Prophet, that each should begin with himself and not wait for the other to step forward, and tells (in sense) that the people in the time of [[person/umar-ibn-abd-al-aziz|Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz]] said we mended what is between us and our Lord so God mended between the wolf and the sheep, and mentions the words and We set his wife right for him ([[passage/quran-21-90|al-Anbiya 90]]). This is the lecturer's view; the account has no chain. (needs_source: the account of Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz's time and the wolf and the sheep is in sense without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:39:00}
- He says in closing that the relation of spouses is complementary and not opposed, and that homes stand on four things: patience, dealing by favour and not strict justice, overlooking the mistake once, twice and thrice, and mutual clearing, removing the clinging residue step by step as a vessel is washed before its food dries. He says it is not allowed for the husband to shun his wife more than three days nor for the wife to shun him more, and gives the example of twenty-five states in Europe united though of different languages and races while our homes do not unite. He mentions that a kind word is better than charity followed by injury ([[passage/quran-2-263|al-Baqara 263]]), and that the sincere one who seeks salvation gives and does not wait ([[passage/quran-76-9|al-Insan 9]]). This is the lecturer's view and generalisation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:46:00}
- He advises making money lawful, fear of God, not wasting time, that the man treat his wife as he would like his daughter to be treated, and that the daughters fear God regarding their husbands, and closes with a prayer. This is the lecturer's prayer. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep46 00:46:30}
